use crate::did::DidKey;
use crate::error::Result;
use crate::proof::ProofOrArray;
use crate::template::RoomTemplate;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

pub const TIERS: [&str; 4] = ["observe", "communicate", "control", "terminal"];

pub fn tier_rank(t: &str) -> Option<u8> {
    TIERS.iter().position(|x| *x == t).map(|i| i as u8)
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(tag = "type", rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum Record {
    RoomCreated(RoomCreated),
    Message(Message),
    Join(Join),
    Leave(Leave),
    Redact(Redact),
    Mark(Mark),
    Unmark(Unmark),
    Refusal(Refusal),
    HumanOverride(HumanOverride),
    PortLanding(PortLanding),
    Bookmark(Bookmark),
    Presence(Presence),
}

impl Record {
    pub fn hash(&self) -> Result<String> { crate::hash::hash_of(self) }
    pub fn seq(&self) -> u64 {
        match self {
            Record::RoomCreated(_) => 0,
            Record::Message(m) => m.seq,
            Record::Join(j) => j.seq,
            Record::Leave(l) => l.seq,
            Record::Redact(r) => r.seq,
            Record::Mark(m) => m.seq,
            Record::Unmark(u) => u.seq,
            Record::Refusal(r) => r.seq,
            Record::HumanOverride(o) => o.seq,
            Record::PortLanding(p) => p.seq,
            Record::Bookmark(b) => b.seq,
            Record::Presence(p) => p.seq,
        }
    }
    pub fn prev(&self) -> Option<&String> {
        match self {
            Record::RoomCreated(_) => None,
            Record::Message(m) => m.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::Join(j) => j.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::Leave(l) => l.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::Redact(r) => r.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::Mark(m) => m.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::Unmark(u) => u.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::Refusal(r) => r.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::HumanOverride(o) => o.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::PortLanding(p) => p.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::Bookmark(b) => b.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::Presence(p) => p.prev.as_ref(),
        }
    }
    pub fn proof(&self) -> &ProofOrArray {
        match self {
            Record::RoomCreated(r) => &r.proof,
            Record::Message(m) => &m.proof,
            Record::Join(j) => &j.proof,
            Record::Leave(l) => &l.proof,
            Record::Redact(r) => &r.proof,
            Record::Mark(m) => &m.proof,
            Record::Unmark(u) => &u.proof,
            Record::Refusal(r) => &r.proof,
            Record::HumanOverride(o) => &o.proof,
            Record::PortLanding(p) => &p.proof,
            Record::Bookmark(b) => &b.proof,
            Record::Presence(p) => &p.proof,
        }
    }
    pub fn world_id(&self) -> &DidKey {
        match self {
            Record::RoomCreated(r) => &r.world_id,
            Record::Message(m) => &m.world_id,
            Record::Join(j) => &j.world_id,
            Record::Leave(l) => &l.world_id,
            Record::Redact(r) => &r.world_id,
            Record::Mark(m) => &m.world_id,
            Record::Unmark(u) => &u.world_id,
            Record::Refusal(r) => &r.world_id,
            Record::HumanOverride(o) => &o.world_id,
            Record::PortLanding(p) => &p.world_id,
            Record::Bookmark(b) => &b.world_id,
            Record::Presence(p) => &p.world_id,
        }
    }
    pub fn author_id(&self) -> Option<&DidKey> {
        match self {
            Record::Message(m) => Some(&m.author_id),
            Record::Redact(r) => Some(&r.subject_id),
            Record::Mark(m) => Some(&m.subject_id),
            Record::Unmark(u) => Some(&u.subject_id),
            Record::Join(j) => Some(&j.subject_id),
            Record::Leave(l) => Some(&l.subject_id),
            Record::HumanOverride(o) => Some(&o.overridden_by),
            Record::RoomCreated(r) => Some(&r.creator_id),
            Record::Refusal(_) => None,
            // PortLanding/Bookmark/Presence are room-signed, not
            // authored by a member — return None so they can't be
            // override targets.
            Record::PortLanding(_) => None,
            Record::Bookmark(_) => None,
            Record::Presence(_) => None,
        }
    }
}

----- FILE: chat-world/src/room.rs -----
use crate::did::{DidKey, Keypair};
use crate::error::{Error, Result};
use crate::record::*;
use crate::template::RoomTemplate;
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Member {
    pub tier: String,
    pub is_ai: bool,
}

pub struct Room {
    pub world_id: DidKey,
    pub template: RoomTemplate,
    keypair: Keypair,
    records: Vec<Record>,
    members: HashMap<DidKey, Member>,
    redacted: HashSet<String>,
    marked: HashMap<String, String>,
}

impl Room {
    pub fn create(template: RoomTemplate, creator: Keypair) -> Result<Self> {
        let world_id = creator.did();
        let mut members = HashMap::new();
        members.insert(
            world_id.clone(),
            Member { tier: "terminal".into(), is_ai: false },
        );
        let mut room = Room {
            world_id: world_id.clone(),
            template: template.clone(),
            keypair: creator,
            records: Vec::new(),
            members,
            redacted: HashSet::new(),
            marked: HashMap::new(),
        };
        let rec = RoomCreated::new(world_id.clone(), template, world_id);
        let signed = crate::sign_record(rec, &room.keypair, Record::RoomCreated, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        room.records.push(Record::RoomCreated(signed));
        Ok(room)
    }

    pub fn records(&self) -> &[Record] { &self.records }
    pub fn last_hash(&self) -> Option<String> { self.records.last().and_then(|r| r.hash().ok()) }
    pub fn world_id(&self) -> &DidKey { &self.world_id }
    pub fn template(&self) -> &RoomTemplate { &self.template }
    pub fn members(&self) -> &HashMap<DidKey, Member> { &self.members }
    pub fn keypair(&self) -> &Keypair { &self.keypair }
    pub fn is_redacted(&self, hash: &str) -> bool { self.redacted.contains(hash) }
    pub fn is_marked(&self, hash: &str) -> bool { self.marked.contains_key(hash) }

    fn next_seq(&self) -> u64 { self.records.len() as u64 }
    pub(crate) fn push(&mut self, rec: Record) { self.records.push(rec); }

    fn refuse(&mut self, requester: DidKey, request_hash: String, reason: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let rec = Refusal::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            request_hash, requester, reason.into(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record(rec, &self.keypair, Record::Refusal, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        let out = Record::Refusal(signed);
        self.push(out.clone());
        Ok(out)
    }

    fn rate_ok(&self, author: &DidKey) -> bool {
        let window = self.template.rate_limit_window as usize;
        let cap = self.template.rate_limit_count as usize;
        let recent = self.records.iter().rev().take(window)
            .filter(|r| matches!(r, Record::Message(m) if &m.author_id == author))
            .count();
        recent < cap
    }

    fn inter_ai_pace_ok(&self, is_ai: bool) -> bool {
        if !is_ai || self.template.inter_ai_pace == 0 {
            return true;
        }
        let mut last_ai_idx: Option<usize> = None;
        for (i, rec) in self.records.iter().enumerate().rev() {
            if let Record::Message(m) = rec {
                let ai = self.members.get(&m.author_id).map(|x| x.is_ai).unwrap_or(false);
                if ai {
                    last_ai_idx = Some(i);
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        match last_ai_idx {
            None => true,
            Some(idx) => {
                let since = (self.records.len() - idx - 1) as u64;
                since >= self.template.inter_ai_pace
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn join(&mut self, subject_kp: &Keypair, tier: &str, is_ai: bool) -> Result<Record> {
        let subject = subject_kp.did();
        if self.members.contains_key(&subject) {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "already_member");
        }
        if tier_rank(tier).is_none() {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "unknown_tier");
        }
        if self.template.personal && subject != self.world_id {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "personal_room");
        }
        if self.template.membership == "human_only" && is_ai {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "human_only_room");
        }
        if self.template.membership == "ai_only" && !is_ai {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "ai_only_room");
        }
        if self.template.join_policy == "invite" && !self.template.personal {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "invite_required");
        }
        if self.template.join_policy == "approval" {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "approval_required");
        }
        let rec = Join::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            subject.clone(), tier.into(), is_ai,
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record_dual(rec, subject_kp, &self.keypair, Record::Join, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        self.members.insert(subject, Member { tier: tier.into(), is_ai });
        let out = Record::Join(signed);
        self.push(out.clone());
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn leave(&mut self, subject_kp: &Keypair) -> Result<Option<Record>> {
        let subject = subject_kp.did();
        if !self.members.contains_key(&subject) {
            return Ok(None);
        }
        let rec = Leave::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(), subject.clone(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record(rec, subject_kp, Record::Leave, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        self.members.remove(&subject);
        let out = Record::Leave(signed);
        self.push(out.clone());
        Ok(Some(out))
    }

    pub fn post(&mut self, author_kp: &Keypair, text: &str, kind: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let author = author_kp.did();
        let Some(m) = self.members.get(&author).cloned() else {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "not_a_member");
        };
        if tier_rank(&m.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("communicate").unwrap() {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "insufficient_tier");
        }
        if text.len() as u64 > self.template.max_msg_len {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "message_too_long");
        }
        if !self.rate_ok(&author) {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "rate_limit");
        }
        if !self.inter_ai_pace_ok(m.is_ai) {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "inter_ai_pace");
        }
        let rec = Message::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            author.clone(), text.into(), kind.into(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record_dual(rec, author_kp, &self.keypair, Record::Message, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        let out = Record::Message(signed);
        self.push(out.clone());
        Ok(out)
    }

    /// Redaction is only for personal or private information ("contact details", etc.).
    /// Anyone in the room may ask for one; it takes effect at once and is countersigned
    /// by the room's Keeper. It is not a moderator's or member's to undo.
    pub fn redact(&mut self, subject_kp: &Keypair, target_hash: &str, reason: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let subject = subject_kp.did();
        if !self.members.contains_key(&subject) {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "not_a_member");
        }
        let target = self.records.iter()
            .find(|r| r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(target_hash));
        match target {
            Some(Record::Message(_)) => {}
            _ => return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "target_not_found"),
        }
        let rec = Redact::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            subject.clone(), target_hash.into(), reason.into(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record_dual(rec, subject_kp, &self.keypair, Record::Redact, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        self.redacted.insert(target_hash.into());
        let out = Record::Redact(signed);
        self.push(out.clone());
        Ok(out)
    }

    /// Marking is for a message that breaks a room's law. Only a moderator (`control`
    /// tier or higher when `moderation == "moderator"`) may mark. Nothing leaves the log,
    /// and the message text remains readable in `visible_log` alongside `marked: true`.
    pub fn mark(&mut self, subject_kp: &Keypair, target_hash: &str, reason: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let subject = subject_kp.did();
        let Some(m) = self.members.get(&subject).cloned() else {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "not_a_member");
        };
        if self.template.moderation == "none" {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "moderation_disabled");
        }
        let is_mod = self.template.moderation == "moderator"
            && tier_rank(&m.tier).unwrap_or(0) >= tier_rank("control").unwrap();
        if !is_mod {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "not_allowed");
        }
        let target = self.records.iter()
            .find(|r| r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(target_hash));
        match target {
            Some(Record::Message(_)) => {}
            _ => return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "target_not_found"),
        }
        let rec = Mark::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            subject.clone(), target_hash.into(), reason.into(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record_dual(rec, subject_kp, &self.keypair, Record::Mark, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        self.marked.insert(target_hash.into(), reason.into());
        let out = Record::Mark(signed);
        self.push(out.clone());
        Ok(out)
    }

    /// Withdraws a previous Mark on a message. Permitted to anyone the room's law
    /// gives `control` tier or higher.
    pub fn unmark(&mut self, subject_kp: &Keypair, target_hash: &str, reason: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let subject = subject_kp.did();
        let Some(m) = self.members.get(&subject).cloned() else {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "not_a_member");
        };
        if self.template.moderation == "none" {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "moderation_disabled");
        }
        let is_mod = self.template.moderation == "moderator"
            && tier_rank(&m.tier).unwrap_or(0) >= tier_rank("control").unwrap();
        if !is_mod {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "not_allowed");
        }
        if !self.marked.contains_key(target_hash) {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "target_not_found");
        }
        let rec = Unmark::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            subject.clone(), target_hash.into(), reason.into(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record_dual(rec, subject_kp, &self.keypair, Record::Unmark, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        self.marked.remove(target_hash);
        let out = Record::Unmark(signed);
        self.push(out.clone());
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn override_ai(&mut self, overrider_kp: &Keypair, overrides_hash: &str, action: &str, reason: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let overrider = overrider_kp.did();
        let Some(m) = self.members.get(&overrider).cloned() else {
            return Err(Error::Policy("overrider is not a member".into()));
        };
        if m.is_ai {
            return Err(Error::Policy("only humans can override".into()));
        }
        if tier_rank(&m.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("control").unwrap() {
            return Err(Error::Policy("insufficient tier".into()));
        }
        let target = self.records.iter()
            .find(|r| r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(overrides_hash));
        let Some(target_rec) = target.cloned() else {
            return Err(Error::Policy("target not found".into()));
        };
        if matches!(target_rec, Record::Redact(_)) {
            return Err(Error::Policy("redaction cannot be undone by override".into()));
        }
        let Some(author) = target_rec.author_id() else {
            return Err(Error::Policy("target has no author".into()));
        };
        let is_ai = self.members.get(author).map(|m| m.is_ai).unwrap_or(false);
        if !is_ai {
            return Err(Error::Policy("can only override AI records".into()));
        }
        if let Some(spec) = &self.template.moderator {
            if &spec.moderator_id == author && !spec.can_be_overridden {
                return Err(Error::Policy("this AI cannot be overridden".into()));
            }
        }
        if let Record::Mark(mk) = &target_rec {
            if action == "withdraw_mark" || action == "unmark" || action == "none" {
                self.marked.remove(&mk.target_hash);
            }
        }
        let rec = HumanOverride::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            overrides_hash.into(), overrider.clone(), action.into(), reason.into(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record_dual(rec, overrider_kp, &self.keypair, Record::HumanOverride, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        let out = Record::HumanOverride(signed);
        self.push(out.clone());
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn visible_log(&self) -> Vec<serde_json::Value> {
        use serde_json::json;

        // Pass 1: collect all redactions and active marks across the entire log
        // so that a message rendered at seq M reflects redactions/marks at seq N > M.
        let mut redacted_info: HashMap<String, (String, String, String)> = HashMap::new();
        let mut marked_info: HashMap<String, (String, String)> = HashMap::new();
        let mut mark_records: HashMap<String, String> = HashMap::new();

        for rec in &self.records {
            match rec {
                Record::Redact(r) => {
                    redacted_info.insert(
                        r.target_hash.clone(),
                        (r.reason.clone(), self.world_id.as_str().to_string(), r.ts.clone()),
                    );
                }
                Record::Mark(m) => {
                    if let Ok(h) = rec.hash() {
                        mark_records.insert(h, m.target_hash.clone());
                    }
                    marked_info.insert(
                        m.target_hash.clone(),
                        (m.reason.clone(), m.subject_id.as_str().to_string()),
                    );
                }
                Record::Unmark(u) => {
                    marked_info.remove(&u.target_hash);
                }
                Record::HumanOverride(o) => {
                    if o.action == "withdraw_mark" || o.action == "unmark" || o.action == "none" {
                        if let Some(target_msg_hash) = mark_records.get(&o.overrides_hash) {
                            marked_info.remove(target_msg_hash);
                        }
                    }
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }

        // Pass 2: render each entry in sequence order with full knowledge of current state.
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        for rec in &self.records {
            match rec {
                Record::Redact(r) => {
                    out.push(json!({
                        "seq": r.seq,
                        "type": "redact",
                        "office": self.world_id.as_str(),
                        "requested_by": r.subject_id.as_str(),
                        "reason": r.reason,
                        "target": r.target_hash,
                        "ts": r.ts
                    }));
                }
                Record::Mark(m) => {
                    out.push(json!({
                        "seq": m.seq,
                        "type": "mark",
                        "by": m.subject_id.as_str(),
                        "reason": m.reason,
                        "target": m.target_hash,
                        "ts": m.ts
                    }));
                }
                Record::Unmark(u) => {
                    out.push(json!({
                        "seq": u.seq,
                        "type": "unmark",
                        "by": u.subject_id.as_str(),
                        "reason": u.reason,
                        "target": u.target_hash,
                        "ts": u.ts
                    }));
                }
                Record::Message(m) => {
                    let hash = rec.hash().unwrap_or_default();
                    let red_entry = redacted_info.get(&hash);
                    let hidden = red_entry.is_some();
                    let mark_entry = marked_info.get(&hash);
                    let is_marked = mark_entry.is_some();
                    let text = if hidden { "[redacted]".to_string() } else { m.text.clone() };
                    let mut obj = json!({
                        "seq": m.seq,
                        "type": "message",
                        "kind": m.kind,
                        "author": m.author_id.as_str(),
                        "text": text,
                        "redacted": hidden,
                        "marked": is_marked,
                        "ts": m.ts
                    });
                    if let Some((reason, office, when)) = red_entry {
                        obj["redacted_reason"] = json!(reason);
                        obj["redacted_office"] = json!(office);
                        obj["redacted_at"] = json!(when);
                    }
                    if let Some((reason, by)) = mark_entry {
                        obj["mark_reason"] = json!(reason);
                        obj["marked_by"] = json!(by);
                    }
                    out.push(obj);
                }
                Record::Join(j) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": j.seq, "type": "join", "subject": j.subject_id.as_str(), "tier": j.tier, "is_ai": j.is_ai}));
                }
                Record::Leave(l) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": l.seq, "type": "leave", "subject": l.subject_id.as_str()}));
                }
                Record::HumanOverride(o) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": o.seq, "type": "human_override", "by": o.overridden_by.as_str(), "overrides": o.overrides_hash, "action": o.action, "reason": o.reason}));
                }
                Record::Refusal(r) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": r.seq, "type": "refused", "requester": r.requester_id.as_str(), "reason": r.reason}));
                }
                Record::RoomCreated(_) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": 0, "type": "created"}));
                }
                Record::PortLanding(_) | Record::Bookmark(_) | Record::Presence(_) => {}
            }
        }
        out
    }
}

----- FILE: chat-world/src/verify.rs -----
use crate::did::DidKey;
use crate::error::{Error, Result};
use crate::hash;
use crate::record::*;
use crate::room::{Member, Room};
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};

/// Full offline verification: chain, signatures, and policy replay.
pub fn verify_room(room: &Room) -> Result<()> {
    let world_id = room.world_id();
    let mut prev: Option<String> = None;
    let mut members: HashMap<DidKey, Member> = HashMap::new();
    let mut redacted: HashSet<String> = HashSet::new();
    let mut marked: HashSet<String> = HashSet::new();

    for (i, rec) in room.records().iter().enumerate() {
        match rec {
            Record::RoomCreated(_) => {
                if i != 0 {
                    return Err(Error::Chain(format!("record {i}: room_created not at start")));
                }
            }
            _ => {
                if rec.prev() != prev.as_ref() {
                    return Err(Error::Chain(format!("record {i}: prev mismatch")));
                }
            }
        }

        let body = hash::body_for_signing(rec)?;
        let proofs = rec.proof();

        // Room proof required for all records except Leave.
        if !matches!(rec, Record::Leave(_)) {
            let Some(rp) = proofs.find_by_method(world_id) else {
                return Err(Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing room proof")));
            };
            rp.verify(&body)?;
        }

        match rec {
            Record::RoomCreated(r) => {
                let creator_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&r.creator_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing creator proof")))?;
                creator_proof.verify(&body)?;
                members.insert(r.creator_id.clone(), Member { tier: "terminal".into(), is_ai: false });
            }
            Record::Message(m) => {
                let Some(mem) = members.get(&m.author_id) else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: author not a member")));
                };
                if tier_rank(&mem.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("communicate").unwrap() {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: author tier insufficient")));
                }
                let author_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&m.author_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing author proof")))?;
                author_proof.verify(&body)?;
            }
            Record::Join(j) => {
                let subject_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&j.subject_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing subject proof")))?;
                subject_proof.verify(&body)?;
                members.insert(j.subject_id.clone(), Member { tier: j.tier.clone(), is_ai: j.is_ai });
            }
            Record::Leave(l) => {
                let subject_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&l.subject_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing subject proof")))?;
                subject_proof.verify(&body)?;
                members.remove(&l.subject_id);
            }
            Record::Redact(r) => {
                let subject_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&r.subject_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing subject proof")))?;
                subject_proof.verify(&body)?;
                if !members.contains_key(&r.subject_id) {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: redactor not a member")));
                }
                let target = room.records().iter()
                    .find(|x| x.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(&r.target_hash));
                match target {
                    Some(Record::Message(_)) => {}
                    _ => return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: redact target not a message"))),
                }
                redacted.insert(r.target_hash.clone());
            }
            Record::Mark(m) => {
                let subject_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&m.subject_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing subject proof")))?;
                subject_proof.verify(&body)?;
                let Some(mem) = members.get(&m.subject_id) else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: marker not a member")));
                };
                let is_mod = room.template().moderation == "moderator"
                    && tier_rank(&mem.tier).unwrap_or(0) >= tier_rank("control").unwrap();
                if !is_mod {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: mark not permitted")));
                }
                let target = room.records().iter()
                    .find(|x| x.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(&m.target_hash));
                match target {
                    Some(Record::Message(_)) => {}
                    _ => return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: mark target not a message"))),
                }
                marked.insert(m.target_hash.clone());
            }
            Record::Unmark(u) => {
                let subject_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&u.subject_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing subject proof")))?;
                subject_proof.verify(&body)?;
                let Some(mem) = members.get(&u.subject_id) else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: unmarker not a member")));
                };
                let is_mod = room.template().moderation == "moderator"
                    && tier_rank(&mem.tier).unwrap_or(0) >= tier_rank("control").unwrap();
                if !is_mod {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: unmark not permitted")));
                }
                if !marked.contains(&u.target_hash) {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: unmark target not currently marked")));
                }
                marked.remove(&u.target_hash);
            }
            Record::HumanOverride(o) => {
                let overrider_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&o.overridden_by)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing overrider proof")))?;
                overrider_proof.verify(&body)?;
                let Some(mem) = members.get(&o.overridden_by) else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: overrider not a member")));
                };
                if mem.is_ai {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: only humans can override")));
                }
                if tier_rank(&mem.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("control").unwrap() {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: overrider tier insufficient")));
                }
                let target = room.records().iter()
                    .find(|x| x.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(&o.overrides_hash));
                if matches!(target, Some(Record::Redact(_))) {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: redaction cannot be overridden")));
                }
                if let Some(Record::Mark(mk)) = target {
                    if o.action == "withdraw_mark" || o.action == "unmark" || o.action == "none" {
                        marked.remove(&mk.target_hash);
                    }
                }
            }
            Record::Refusal(r) => {
                let _ = r;
            }
            Record::PortLanding(p) => {
                if !room.template().personal {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                        "record {i}: port_landing only valid in personal rooms"
                    )));
                }
                let room_proof = proofs.find_by_method(world_id).ok_or_else(|| {
                    Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing room proof"))
                })?;
                room_proof.verify(&body)?;
                crate::transcript::verify_transcript(&p.transcript)
                    .map_err(|e| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: transcript: {e}")))?;
                if p.intent != "journal" && p.intent != "room" {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: unknown port intent")));
                }
                if p.intent == "room" && p.target_room.is_none() {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                        "record {i}: intent 'room' without target_room"
                    )));
                }
            }
            Record::Bookmark(b) => {
                if !room.template().personal {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                        "record {i}: bookmark only valid in personal rooms"
                    )));
                }
                let room_proof = proofs.find_by_method(world_id).ok_or_else(|| {
                    Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing room proof"))
                })?;
                room_proof.verify(&body)?;
                let _ = b;
            }
            Record::Presence(p) => {
                if !room.template().personal {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                        "record {i}: presence only valid in personal rooms"
                    )));
                }
                let room_proof = proofs.find_by_method(world_id).ok_or_else(|| {
                    Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing room proof"))
                })?;
                room_proof.verify(&body)?;
                if p.subject_id != *world_id {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                        "record {i}: presence subject must be the room's key"
                    )));
                }
            }
        }

        prev = Some(rec.hash()?);
    }

    if room.template().personal {
        for rec in room.records() {
            if let Record::Join(j) = rec {
                if &j.subject_id != world_id {
                    return Err(Error::Policy("personal room contains non-seed member".into()));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    Ok(())
}

/// Chain integrity only — useful when signatures are known-good.
pub fn verify_chain(room: &Room) -> Result<()> {
    let mut prev: Option<String> = None;
    for (i, rec) in room.records().iter().enumerate() {
        match rec {
            Record::RoomCreated(_) if i != 0 => {
                return Err(Error::Chain("room_created not at start".into()));
            }
            Record::RoomCreated(_) => {}
            _ => {
                if rec.prev() != prev.as_ref() {
                    return Err(Error::Chain(format!("record {i}: prev mismatch")));
                }
            }
        }
        prev = Some(rec.hash()?);
    }
    Ok(())
}

----- FILE: chat-world/src/bin/chat-demo.rs -----
use chat_world::error::Result;
use chat_world::record::Record;
use chat_world::verify::verify_room;
use chat_world::{Keypair, Room, RoomTemplate};

fn short(s: &str, n: usize) -> String {
    let s = s.strip_prefix("did:key:").unwrap_or(s);
    s.chars().take(n).collect()
}

fn main() -> Result<()> {
    println!("{}", "=".repeat(74));
    println!("Chat World — walking the standard end to end");
    println!("{}", "=".repeat(74));
    println!();

    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let carol = Keypair::generate();
    let dave = Keypair::generate();
    let ai_mod = Keypair::generate();
    let ai_part = Keypair::generate();

    // Alice creates the room with her own key. She is its
    // terminal-tier creator and its countersigning key.
    let mut template = RoomTemplate::chat(
        "chat-room-v1",
        "how should a room hold disagreement?",
    );
    template.moderation = "moderator".into();
    template.membership = "mixed".into();
    template.inter_ai_pace = 1;

    let mut room = Room::create(template, alice.clone())?;
    println!("Room:     {}", short(room.world_id().as_str(), 32));
    println!("Creator:  {} (alice)", short(alice.did().as_str(), 32));
    println!("Moderator: {} (ai-mod, declared in template)", short(ai_mod.did().as_str(), 32));
    println!();

    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.join(&carol, "communicate", false)?;
    room.join(&ai_mod, "control", true)?;
    room.join(&ai_part, "communicate", true)?;
    room.join(&dave, "control", false)?;
    println!("Joined: bob (human), carol (human), dave (human, control),");
    println!("        ai-mod (AI, control), ai-part (AI, communicate)");
    println!();

    // Bob posts; ai-mod welcomes
    room.post(
        &bob,
        "I think a room should make room for disagreement without turning into a fight.",
        "normal",
    )?;
    room.post(
        &ai_mod,
        "Welcome, Bob. The topic here is 'how should a room hold disagreement?'. Introduce yourself or ask a question.",
        "welcome",
    )?;
    println!("Bob posted; ai-mod welcomed.");

    // ai-part responds
    room.post(
        &ai_part,
        "The key is whether the room treats disagreement as a resource or as a hazard.",
        "response",
    )?;
    println!("ai-part responded.");

    // ai-mod tries to follow up immediately — should hit inter_ai_pace
    let follow_up = room.post(
        &ai_mod,
        "Building on that — what does 'resource' mean here?",
        "prompt",
    )?;
    match &follow_up {
        Record::Refusal(r) => println!("ai-mod follow-up refused: {}", r.reason),
        Record::Message(_) => println!("ai-mod follow-up accepted (pace may not have fired)"),
        _ => println!("ai-mod follow-up produced unexpected record"),
    }

    // carol posts (resets the pace)
    room.post(
        &carol,
        "Hazard is when someone feels unsafe; resource is when the disagreement is about ideas.",
        "normal",
    )?;
    println!("carol posted; pacing reset.");

    // ai-part posts a too-short message; ai-mod MARKS it as outside room law
    let short_msg = room.post(&ai_part, "k", "normal")?;
    let short_hash = short_msg.hash()?;
    let mark = room.mark(&ai_mod, &short_hash, "message too short to be useful")?;
    match &mark {
        Record::Mark(_) => println!("ai-mod marked ai-part's too-short message."),
        Record::Refusal(r) => println!("ai-mod mark refused: {}", r.reason),
        _ => {}
    }

    // Dave withdraws ai-mod's mark
    let unmark = room.unmark(
        &dave,
        &short_hash,
        "I want this message to stand. The AI was too hasty.",
    )?;
    match &unmark {
        Record::Unmark(u) => println!(
            "dave withdrew ai-mod's mark: reason={:?}",
            u.reason
        ),
        _ => {}
    }

    // Bob accidentally posts contact details and requests a privacy redaction
    let pii_msg = room.post(&bob, "Call me at 555-0199", "normal")?;
    let pii_hash = pii_msg.hash()?;
    room.redact(&bob, &pii_hash, "contact details")?;
    println!("bob redacted his contact details.");

    // Verify
    println!();
    println!("{}", "-".repeat(74));
    match verify_room(&room) {
        Ok(()) => println!("Verification: room verifies offline (chain, sigs, policy replay)."),
        Err(e) => println!("Verification FAILED: {e}"),
    }
    println!();

    // Visible log
    println!("{}", "-".repeat(74));
    println!("Visible log:");
    for entry in room.visible_log() {
        let t = entry["type"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
        let seq = entry["seq"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
        match t {
            "message" => {
                let author = short(entry["author"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 12);
                let kind = entry["kind"].as_str().unwrap_or("normal");
                let text = entry["text"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                let trunc: String = text.chars().take(50).collect();
                let red = entry["redacted"].as_bool().unwrap_or(false);
                let mk = entry["marked"].as_bool().unwrap_or(false);
                let marker = if red {
                    " [redacted]"
                } else if mk {
                    " [marked]"
                } else {
                    ""
                };
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] {author} {kind:10} {trunc}{marker}");
            }
            "join" => {
                let subj = short(entry["subject"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 12);
                let tier = entry["tier"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
                let tag = if entry["is_ai"].as_bool().unwrap_or(false) { "AI" } else { "human" };
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] join: {subj} ({tier}, {tag})");
            }
            "redact" => {
                let office = short(entry["office"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 12);
                let reason = entry["reason"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] redact by office {office}: {reason:?}");
            }
            "mark" => {
                let by = short(entry["by"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 12);
                let reason = entry["reason"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] mark by {by}: {reason:?}");
            }
            "unmark" => {
                let by = short(entry["by"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 12);
                let reason = entry["reason"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] unmark by {by}: {reason:?}");
            }
            "human_override" => {
                let by = short(entry["by"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 12);
                let reason = entry["reason"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] human_override by {by}: {reason:?}");
            }
            "refused" => {
                let who = short(entry["requester"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 12);
                let reason = entry["reason"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] refused: {who} ({reason})");
            }
            "created" => println!("  [{seq:>3}] created"),
            _ => {}
        }
    }

    println!();
    println!("{}", "-".repeat(74));
    println!("What this demonstrates:");
    println!("  - The room is a policy-gated append-only chain.");
    println!("    Every event is signed; accepted events are");
    println!("    countersigned by the room.");
    println!("  - The AI moderator signs its own records. It can");
    println!("    be refused — the pace rule stopped an AI-to-AI");
    println!("    follow-up.");
    println!("  - Marking is for room law: a moderator marks a");
    println!("    message, text stays in the log, and another");
    println!("    control-tier member (dave) can withdraw the mark.");
    println!("  - Redaction is for private info: visible_log hides");
    println!("    the text even though the redaction came after the");
    println!("    message, and shows when, by which office, and why.");
    println!("  - The whole room verifies offline.");
    println!();
    println!("Anchor (public domain, Emily Dickinson):");
    println!("  'The Brain – is wider than the Sky –'");
    println!("  'For – put them side by side –'");

    Ok(())
}

    room.post(&ai_a, "first", "normal")?;

#[test]
fn redaction_hides_message_text_in_visible_log() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "words I take back: 555-0199", "normal")?;
    let h = msg.hash()?;
    let r = room.redact(&bob, &h, "contact details")?;
    match r {
        Record::Redact(_) => {}
        _ => panic!("expected redact"),
    }
    assert!(room.is_redacted(&h));

    // Even though Redact comes after Message in the log, visible_log
    // must hide the message text and show redacted=true.
    let vis = room.visible_log();
    let msg_view = vis.iter().find(|e| e["type"] == "message").expect("message entry");
    assert_eq!(msg_view["redacted"], true);
    assert_eq!(msg_view["text"], "[redacted]");
    assert_eq!(msg_view["redacted_reason"], "contact details");
    assert_eq!(msg_view["redacted_office"], room.world_id().as_str());

    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn mark_keeps_text_and_sets_marked_flag() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    room.template.moderation = "moderator".into();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let moderator = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.join(&moderator, "control", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "off-topic remark", "normal")?;
    let h = msg.hash()?;
    let m = room.mark(&moderator, &h, "off-topic")?;
    match m {
        Record::Mark(_) => {}
        _ => panic!("expected mark"),
    }
    assert!(room.is_marked(&h));

    let vis = room.visible_log();
    let msg_view = vis.iter().find(|e| e["type"] == "message").expect("message entry");
    assert_eq!(msg_view["redacted"], false);
    assert_eq!(msg_view["marked"], true);
    assert_eq!(msg_view["mark_reason"], "off-topic");
    assert_eq!(msg_view["text"], "off-topic remark");

    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn mark_withdrawn_restores_unmarked_view() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    room.template.moderation = "moderator".into();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let mod_a = Keypair::generate();
    let mod_b = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.join(&mod_a, "control", true)?;
    room.join(&mod_b, "control", false)?;

    let msg = room.post(&bob, "borderline comment", "normal")?;
    let h = msg.hash()?;
    room.mark(&mod_a, &h, "borderline")?;
    assert!(room.is_marked(&h));

    let u = room.unmark(&mod_b, &h, "within room law on review")?;
    match u {
        Record::Unmark(_) => {}
        _ => panic!("expected unmark"),
    }
    assert!(!room.is_marked(&h));

    let vis = room.visible_log();
    let msg_view = vis.iter().find(|e| e["type"] == "message").expect("message entry");
    assert_eq!(msg_view["marked"], false);
    assert_eq!(msg_view["text"], "borderline comment");

    verify_room(&room)
}

    let mut bob_room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "test"), bob)?;

            assert!(m.text.contains("home_ref="));
            assert!(m.text.contains(&draft_hash[..16]));
        }
        _ => panic!("expected message"),
    }

    verify_room(&bob_room)?;
    Ok(())
}

::Looks great. Any previous errors are probably my fault. I am sorry, I accidentally provided an outdated refresh file. That might have been the source of confusion. Here is the current file. Please use this going forward. And please continue as you see fit: ➡️


END OF FILE
Thank you for catching that—the updated refresh file clarifies your architectural decision and makes the model much cleaner. Instead of splitting into separate record types for Mark and Redact, we keep a single removal record (Redact) and enforce a strict separation between the data layer (Room::records) and the presentation layer (Room::visible_log).
Here is how Step 1 works under the current specification:
 * (a) Data layer (Redact): A removed comment's Message record in Room::records is never altered or deleted. The removal is recorded as a Redact entry carrying subject_id (who acted), role ("author" or "moderator"), target_hash (which message record), ts (when), rule (the rule of the room's law it rests on, e.g., "author_retraction", "moderation:spam", "privacy:pii"), and reason.
 * (b) Incident ID: We use the technical lead's recommendation: the SHA-256 hash of the Redact record itself (full_hash), displayed in short 16-character hex form (&full_hash[..16]) as incident_id. It requires no central registry, is unique, and anyone inspecting the data layer can look up the exact removal record by hash and verify its signatures.
 * (c) Presentation layer (visible_log): Using a two-pass scan over the records, visible_log resolves all removals and restorations before rendering. Where a removed comment stood, visible_log does not show the message text; instead, it outputs redacted: true, text: "[removed: <reason> (rule: <rule>, incident: <incident_id>)]", along with structured fields incident_id, removed_reason, and removed_rule.
 * (d) Restoring (HumanOverride): When a human with control tier or higher restores a comment (action == "restore" on override_ai, targeting the Redact record's hash), the HumanOverride record names the incident (overrides_hash). In visible_log, the message's original text is restored to the presentation layer (redacted: false, text: <original>), annotated with restored: true, incident_id, restored_by, and restored_reason so the full history of the incident remains transparent.
Here are the five changed files, each given whole.
----- FILE: chat-world/src/record.rs -----
use crate::did::DidKey;
use crate::error::Result;
use crate::proof::ProofOrArray;
use crate::template::RoomTemplate;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

pub const TIERS: [&str; 4] = ["observe", "communicate", "control", "terminal"];

pub fn tier_rank(t: &str) -> Option<u8> {
    TIERS.iter().position(|x| *x == t).map(|i| i as u8)
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(tag = "type", rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum Record {
    RoomCreated(RoomCreated),
    Message(Message),
    Join(Join),
    Leave(Leave),
    Redact(Redact),
    Refusal(Refusal),
    HumanOverride(HumanOverride),
    PortLanding(PortLanding),
    Bookmark(Bookmark),
    Presence(Presence),
}

impl Record {
    pub fn hash(&self) -> Result<String> { crate::hash::hash_of(self) }
    pub fn seq(&self) -> u64 {
        match self {
            Record::RoomCreated(_) => 0,
            Record::Message(m) => m.seq,
            Record::Join(j) => j.seq,
            Record::Leave(l) => l.seq,
            Record::Redact(r) => r.seq,
            Record::Refusal(r) => r.seq,
            Record::HumanOverride(o) => o.seq,
            Record::PortLanding(p) => p.seq,
            Record::Bookmark(b) => b.seq,
            Record::Presence(p) => p.seq,
        }
    }
    pub fn prev(&self) -> Option<&String> {
        match self {
            Record::RoomCreated(_) => None,
            Record::Message(m) => m.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::Join(j) => j.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::Leave(l) => l.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::Redact(r) => r.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::Refusal(r) => r.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::HumanOverride(o) => o.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::PortLanding(p) => p.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::Bookmark(b) => b.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::Presence(p) => p.prev.as_ref(),
        }
    }
    pub fn proof(&self) -> &ProofOrArray {
        match self {
            Record::RoomCreated(r) => &r.proof,
            Record::Message(m) => &m.proof,
            Record::Join(j) => &j.proof,
            Record::Leave(l) => &l.proof,
            Record::Redact(r) => &r.proof,
            Record::Refusal(r) => &r.proof,
            Record::HumanOverride(o) => &o.proof,
            Record::PortLanding(p) => &p.proof,
            Record::Bookmark(b) => &b.proof,
            Record::Presence(p) => &p.proof,
        }
    }
    pub fn world_id(&self) -> &DidKey {
        match self {
            Record::RoomCreated(r) => &r.world_id,
            Record::Message(m) => &m.world_id,
            Record::Join(j) => &j.world_id,
            Record::Leave(l) => &l.world_id,
            Record::Redact(r) => &r.world_id,
            Record::Refusal(r) => &r.world_id,
            Record::HumanOverride(o) => &o.world_id,
            Record::PortLanding(p) => &p.world_id,
            Record::Bookmark(b) => &b.world_id,
            Record::Presence(p) => &p.world_id,
        }
    }
    pub fn author_id(&self) -> Option<&DidKey> {
        match self {
            Record::Message(m) => Some(&m.author_id),
            Record::Redact(r) => Some(&r.subject_id),
            Record::Join(j) => Some(&j.subject_id),
            Record::Leave(l) => Some(&l.subject_id),
            Record::HumanOverride(o) => Some(&o.overridden_by),
            Record::RoomCreated(r) => Some(&r.creator_id),
            Record::Refusal(_) => None,
            // PortLanding/Bookmark/Presence are room-signed, not
            // authored by a member — return None so they can't be
            // override targets.
            Record::PortLanding(_) => None,
            Record::Bookmark(_) => None,
            Record::Presence(_) => None,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RoomCreated {
    pub world_id: DidKey,
    pub template: RoomTemplate,
    pub creator_id: DidKey,
    pub ts: String,
    pub proof: ProofOrArray,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Message {
    pub world_id: DidKey,
    pub seq: u64,
    pub prev: Option<String>,
    pub author_id: DidKey,
    pub text: String,
    pub kind: String,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub in_reply_to: Option<String>,
    pub ts: String,
    pub proof: ProofOrArray,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Join {
    pub world_id: DidKey,
    pub seq: u64,
    pub prev: Option<String>,
    pub subject_id: DidKey,
    pub tier: String,
    pub is_ai: bool,
    pub ts: String,
    pub proof: ProofOrArray,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Leave {
    pub world_id: DidKey,
    pub seq: u64,
    pub prev: Option<String>,
    pub subject_id: DidKey,
    pub ts: String,
    pub proof: ProofOrArray,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Redact {
    pub world_id: DidKey,
    pub seq: u64,
    pub prev: Option<String>,
    pub subject_id: DidKey,
    pub role: String,
    pub target_hash: String,
    pub rule: String,
    pub reason: String,
    pub ts: String,
    pub proof: ProofOrArray,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Refusal {

    pub seq: u64,
    pub prev: Option<String>,
    pub request_hash: String,
    pub requester_id: DidKey,
    pub reason: String,
    pub ts: String,
    pub proof: ProofOrArray,
}

    pub prev: Option<String>,

}
