fn commit_draft_links_home_and_room() -> Result<()> {
    let home_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob_room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let mut home = HomeRoom::create(home_kp, alice.clone(), "test home")?;
    let mut bob_room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "test"), bob_room_kp, &bob)?;
    bob_room.join(&alice, "communicate", false)?;
    let draft = home.draft("hello", Some(bob_room.world_id()))?;
    let draft_hash = draft.hash()?;
    let committed = home.commit_draft_to_room(&draft_hash, &mut bob_room)?;
    match committed {
        Record::Message(m) => {
            assert!(m.text.contains("home_ref="));
            assert!(m.text.contains(&draft_hash[..16]));
        }
        _ => panic!("expected message"),
    }
    verify_room(home.room())?;
    verify_room(&bob_room)?;
    Ok(())
}

File: chat-world/src/bin/chat-demo.rs
use chat_world::error::Result;
use chat_world::record::Record;
use chat_world::verify::verify_room;
use chat_world::{Keypair, Room, RoomTemplate};

fn short(s: &str, n: usize) -> String {
    let s = s.strip_prefix("did:key:").unwrap_or(s);
    s.chars().take(n).collect()
}

fn main() -> Result<()> {
    println!("{}", "=".repeat(74));
    println!("Chat World — walking the standard end to end");
    println!("{}", "=".repeat(74));
    println!();

    let room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let carol = Keypair::generate();
    let dave = Keypair::generate();
    let ai_mod = Keypair::generate();
    let ai_part = Keypair::generate();

    let mut template = RoomTemplate::chat(
        "chat-room-v1",
        "how should a room hold disagreement?",
    );
    template.moderation = "moderator".into();
    template.membership = "mixed".into();
    template.inter_ai_pace = 1;

    let mut room = Room::create(template, room_kp, &alice)?;
    println!("Room:      {} (room's own key)", short(room.world_id().as_str(), 32));
    println!("Creator:   {} (alice, joined as first member)", short(alice.did().as_str(), 32));
    println!("Moderator: {} (ai-mod, declared in template)", short(ai_mod.did().as_str(), 32));
    println!();

    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.join(&carol, "communicate", false)?;
    room.join(&ai_mod, "control", true)?;
    room.join(&ai_part, "communicate", true)?;
    room.join(&dave, "control", false)?;
    println!("Joined: bob (human), carol (human), dave (human, control),");
    println!("        ai-mod (AI, control), ai-part (AI, communicate)");
    println!();

    room.post(
        &bob,
        "I think a room should make room for disagreement without turning into a fight.",
        "normal",
    )?;
    room.post(
        &ai_mod,
        "Welcome, Bob. The topic here is 'how should a room hold disagreement?'. Introduce yourself or ask a question.",
        "welcome",
    )?;
    println!("Bob posted; ai-mod welcomed.");

    room.post(
        &ai_part,
        "The key is whether the room treats disagreement as a resource or as a hazard.",
        "response",
    )?;
    println!("ai-part responded.");

    let follow_up = room.post(
        &ai_mod,
        "Building on that — what does 'resource' mean here?",
        "prompt",
    )?;
    match &follow_up {
        Record::Refusal(r) => println!("ai-mod follow-up refused: {}", r.reason),
        Record::Message(_) => println!("ai-mod follow-up accepted (pace may not have fired)"),
        _ => println!("ai-mod follow-up produced unexpected record"),
    }

    room.post(
        &carol,
        "Hazard is when someone feels unsafe; resource is when the disagreement is about ideas.",
        "normal",
    )?;
    println!("carol posted; pacing reset.");

    let short_msg = room.post(&ai_part, "k", "normal")?;
    let short_hash = short_msg.hash()?;
    let redact = room.redact_with_rule(
        &ai_mod,
        &short_hash,
        "room_law:min_substance",
        "message too short to be useful",
    )?;
    let redact_hash = redact.hash()?;
    match &redact {
        Record::Redact(_) => println!(
            "ai-mod removed ai-part's too-short message (incident={}).",
            short(&redact_hash, 16)
        ),
        Record::Refusal(r) => println!("ai-mod removal refused: {}", r.reason),
        _ => {}
    }

    let ov = room.override_ai(
        &dave,
        &redact_hash,
        "restore",
        "I want this message to stand. The AI was too hasty.",
    )?;
    match &ov {
        Record::HumanOverride(o) => println!(
            "dave restored incident {}: action={}, reason={:?}",
            short(&o.overrides_hash, 16),
            o.action,
            o.reason
        ),
        _ => {}
    }

    let bob_oops = room.post(&bob, "words I take back", "normal")?;
    let bob_oops_hash = bob_oops.hash()?;
    let bob_red = room.redact(&bob, &bob_oops_hash, "my bad")?;
    println!(
        "bob removed his own comment (incident={}).",
        short(&bob_red.hash()?, 16)
    );

    // Also checkpoint log head
    room.checkpoint_head()?;

    println!();
    println!("{}", "-".repeat(74));
    match verify_room(&room) {
        Ok(()) => println!("Verification: room verifies offline (chain, sigs, policy replay)."),
        Err(e) => println!("Verification FAILED: {e}"),
    }
    println!();

    println!("{}", "-".repeat(74));
    println!("Visible log:");
    for entry in room.visible_log() {
        let t = entry["type"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
        let seq = entry["seq"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
        match t {
            "message" => {
                let author = short(entry["author"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 12);
                let kind = entry["kind"].as_str().unwrap_or("normal");
                let text = entry["text"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                let trunc: String = text.chars().take(60).collect();
                let red = entry["redacted"].as_bool().unwrap_or(false);
                let rest = entry["restored"].as_bool().unwrap_or(false);
                let marker = if red {
                    " [removed]"
                } else if rest {
                    " [restored]"
                } else {
                    ""
                };
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] {author} {kind:10} {trunc}{marker}");
            }
            "join" => {
                let subj = short(entry["subject"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 12);
                let tier = entry["tier"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
                let tag = if entry["is_ai"].as_bool().unwrap_or(false) { "AI" } else { "human" };
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] join: {subj} ({tier}, {tag})");
            }
            "redact" => {
                let by = short(entry["by"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 12);
                let role = entry["role"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
                let rule = entry["rule"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
                let inc = entry["incident_id"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
                let reason = entry["reason"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] redact (incident={inc}) by {by} ({role}, {rule}): {reason:?}");
            }
            "human_override" => {
                let by = short(entry["by"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 12);
                let inc = entry["incident_id"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
                let action = entry["action"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
                let reason = entry["reason"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] human_override (incident={inc}, {action}) by {by}: {reason:?}");
            }
            "log_head" => {
                let length = entry["length"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] log_head (batched checkpoint covering length {length})");
            }
            "refused" => {
                let who = short(entry["requester"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 12);
                let reason = entry["reason"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] refused: {who} ({reason})");
            }
            "created" => println!("  [{seq:>3}] created"),
            _ => {}
        }
    }

    println!();
    println!("{}", "-".repeat(74));
    println!("What this demonstrates:");
    println!("  - The room has its own key, separate from its creator's key.");
    println!("  - Redaction leaves the data layer intact while updating the presentation layer.");
    println!("  - Log heads can batch sign checkpoints across multiple entries.");
    println!("  - The whole room verifies offline.");
    println!();
    println!("Anchor (public domain, Emily Dickinson):");
    println!("  'The Brain – is wider than the Sky –'");
    println!("  'For – put them side by side –'");

    Ok(())
}

File: chat-world/src/bin/home-demo.rs
use chat_world::commons::{verify_commons, Commons};
use chat_world::error::Result;
use chat_world::home::HomeRoom;
use chat_world::record::Record;
use chat_world::transcript::{sign_transcript, TranscriptEntry};
use chat_world::verify::verify_room;
use chat_world::{Keypair, Room, RoomTemplate};

fn short(s: &str, n: usize) -> String {
    let s = s.strip_prefix("did:key:").unwrap_or(s);
    s.chars().take(n).collect()
}

fn check_home_ref(rec: &Record, home: &HomeRoom) -> Option<String> {
    let text = match rec {
        Record::Message(m) => &m.text,
        _ => return None,
    };
    let idx = text.find("home_ref=")?;
    let rest = &text[idx + 9..];
    let prefix: String = rest.chars().take(16).collect();
    let found = home
        .room()
        .records()
        .iter()
        .any(|r| r.hash().map(|h| h.starts_with(&prefix)).unwrap_or(false));
    Some(format!("home_ref={prefix}: {}", if found { "found" } else { "MISSING" }))
}

fn main() -> Result<()> {
    println!("{}", "=".repeat(74));
    println!("Home Room + Commons — the user's journey outward");
    println!("{}", "=".repeat(74));
    println!();

    let alice_home_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob_room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let commons_kp = Keypair::generate();

    let mut home = HomeRoom::create(alice_home_kp, alice.clone(), "alice's home")?;
    println!("Alice's home:  {} (home's own key)", short(home.room().world_id().as_str(), 32));
    println!("  owner:       {} (alice)", short(home.user_id().as_str(), 32));
    println!("  template:    {}", home.room().template().template_id);
    println!("  personal:    {}", home.room().template().personal);
    println!();

    home.journal("First entry. I want to understand how composition works at three levels.")?;
    home.journal("Note: the parent supplies, the child decides.")?;
    println!("Journals: 2 entries");

    let bob_template = RoomTemplate::chat("fractal-worlds", "fractal worlds");
    let mut bob_room = Room::create(bob_template, bob_room_kp, &bob)?;
    println!("Bob's room:    {} (room's own key)", short(bob_room.world_id().as_str(), 32));

    let mut commons = Commons::new(commons_kp);
    commons.register_room(&bob_room, &bob, None)?;
    println!("Commons:       {}", short(commons.world_id.as_str(), 32));
    println!("  registered {} room(s)", commons.records().len());
    println!();

    let draft = home.draft(
        "I've been thinking about the composition rule. What if a plant could refuse even when the garden gives it water?",
        Some(bob_room.world_id()),
    )?;
    let draft_hash = draft.hash()?;
    println!("Draft:     {}", short(&draft_hash, 20));

    let entries = vec![
        TranscriptEntry {
            speaker: "user".into(),
            ts: chat_world::now(),
            text: "What is composition in fractal worlds?".into(),
        },
        TranscriptEntry {
            speaker: "assistant".into(),
            ts: chat_world::now(),
            text: "The parent delegates the child's law to the child. It enforces only interaction rules.".into(),
        },
    ];
    let transcript = sign_transcript("claude.ai session, 2026-11-15", entries, &alice)?;
    let port = home.port(&transcript, "room", Some(bob_room.world_id()))?;
    let port_hash = port.hash()?;
    println!("Port:      {} (from {})", short(&port_hash, 20), transcript.source_label);

    home.bookmark(&bob_room, "good conversation, come back")?;
    home.announce_presence(&[bob_room.world_id().clone()], 300)?;
    println!("Bookmark + presence recorded.");
    println!();

    bob_room.join(&alice, "communicate", false)?;
    println!("Alice joined Bob's room (tier: communicate).");

    let committed_draft = home.commit_draft_to_room(&draft_hash, &mut bob_room)?;
    println!("Committed draft: {}", short(&committed_draft.hash()?, 20));
    let committed_port = home.commit_port_to_room(&port_hash, &mut bob_room)?;
    println!("Committed port:  {}", short(&committed_port.hash()?, 20));
    println!();

    println!("{}", "-".repeat(74));
    println!("Verification:");
    match verify_room(home.room()) {
        Ok(()) => println!("  home:    verifies (chain, sigs, policy)"),
        Err(e) => println!("  home:    FAILED: {e}"),
    }
    match verify_room(&bob_room) {
        Ok(()) => println!("  bob's:   verifies"),
        Err(e) => println!("  bob's:   FAILED: {e}"),
    }
    match verify_commons(&commons) {
        Ok(()) => println!("  commons: verifies"),
        Err(e) => println!("  commons: FAILED: {e}"),
    }
    println!();

    println!("Cross-references (home_ref in Bob's room → home):");
    for (label, rec) in [("draft", &committed_draft), ("port", &committed_port)] {
        match check_home_ref(rec, &home) {
            Some(msg) => println!("  {label}: {msg}"),
            None => println!("  {label}: no home_ref found"),
        }
    }
    println!();

    let export = home.export()?;
    let count = export["records"].as_array().map(|a| a.len()).unwrap_or(0);
    println!("Home export: {} records, portable JSON", count);
    println!();

    println!("{}", "-".repeat(74));
    println!("Bob's room — visible log:");
    for entry in bob_room.visible_log() {
        let t = entry["type"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
        let seq = entry["seq"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
        match t {
            "message" => {
                let author = short(entry["author"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 10);
                let kind = entry["kind"].as_str().unwrap_or("normal");
                let text = entry["text"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                let trunc: String = text.chars().take(48).collect();
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] {author} {kind:8} {trunc}");
            }
            "join" => {
                let subj = short(entry["subject"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 10);
                let tier = entry["tier"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] join: {subj} ({tier})");
            }
            "created" => println!("  [{seq:>3}] created"),
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    println!();

    println!("What this establishes:");
    println!("  - Both the home room and Bob's room have their own");
    println!("    world keys, separate from Alice's and Bob's keys.");
    println!("  - The home is a personal room. Only its creator key");
    println!("    can be a member. Journals, drafts, ports,");
    println!("    bookmarks, and presence all live in one chain.");
    println!("  - Porting an external conversation is an attestation.");
    println!("  - Committing to a room binds the two logs via home_ref.");
    println!("  - The Commons is a directory, not a gatekeeper.");
    println!("  - All three logs verify offline.");
    println!();
    println!("Anchor (public domain, Emily Dickinson):");
    println!("  'I'm Nobody! Who are you?'");
    println!("  'Are you – Nobody – too?'");

    Ok(())
}

➡️


::Perfect. But before moving forward, here is the updated collaboration file. Please work from this. Thoughts?


END OF FILE
The architectural insight in this brief goes right to the heart of A Theory of Opposites: a verification function that only ever inspects structs constructed by its own trusted constructors is merely checking an internal invariant, not verifying an external claim. Decoupling the published artifact (RoomLog) from the capability-holding state machine (Room) turns "verifies offline" from a slogan into a testable boundary.
Here is how Step 1 is structured across the five files below:
 * Where RoomLog lives: RoomLog is defined in src/room.rs and re-exported at the crate root in src/lib.rs (pub use room::{Member, Room, RoomLog};) and in src/verify.rs (pub use crate::room::RoomLog;), so callers and tests can import it from either chat_world::RoomLog, chat_world::room::RoomLog, or chat_world::verify::RoomLog.
 * Untrusted template and world_id checks in verify_log: verify_log trusts nothing in the outer RoomLog wrapper. When it reads the first record (Record::RoomCreated(rc)), it checks that rc.world_id == log.world_id (returning Error::Verification on mismatch) and that rc.template.hash()? == log.template.hash()? (returning Error::Verification("room_created template does not match log template") if they differ). It also checks on every record i that rec.world_id() == &log.world_id, and uses rc.template (the signed template inside room_created) as the authoritative law throughout replay.
 * Chain and head verification on RoomLog: verify_chain is joined by pub fn verify_log_chain(log: &RoomLog) -> Result<()> (with verify_chain(room) delegating to it), and RoomLog::verify_head(&self, head: &WorldHead) -> Result<()> is added in src/head.rs so Room::verify_head delegates to self.log().verify_head(head).
 * One check path: verify_room(room) is now simply verify_log(&room.log()).
src/lib.rs
pub mod error;
pub mod hash;
pub mod did;
pub mod proof;
pub mod template;
pub mod record;
pub mod room;
pub mod verify;
pub mod transcript;
pub mod home;
pub mod commons;
pub mod head;
pub mod attestation;

pub use did::{DidKey, Keypair};
pub use error::{Error, Result};
pub use head::WorldHead;
pub use proof::{Proof, ProofOrArray, ProofPurpose};
pub use room::{Member, Room, RoomLog};
pub use template::{ModeratorSpec, RoomTemplate};
pub use attestation::{attest_runtime, verify_attestation, RuntimeAttestation};

pub fn now() -> String {
    chrono::Utc::now().to_rfc3339_opts(chrono::SecondsFormat::Millis, true)
}

pub(crate) fn sign_record<T, F>(
    mut rec: T,
    signer: &Keypair,
    wrap: fn(T) -> record::Record,
    set_proof: F,
) -> Result<T>
where
    T: serde::Serialize + Clone,
    F: FnOnce(&mut T, ProofOrArray),
{
    let body = hash::body_for_signing(&wrap(rec.clone()))?;
    let sig = signer.sign(&body);
    let proof = ProofOrArray::Single(Proof::create(signer, &sig));
    set_proof(&mut rec, proof);
    Ok(rec)
}

pub(crate) fn sign_record_dual<T, F>(
    mut rec: T,
    subject: &Keypair,
    room: &Keypair,
    wrap: fn(T) -> record::Record,
    set_proof: F,
) -> Result<T>
where
    T: serde::Serialize + Clone,
    F: FnOnce(&mut T, ProofOrArray),
{
    let body = hash::body_for_signing(&wrap(rec.clone()))?;
    let subj_sig = subject.sign(&body);
    let room_sig = room.sign(&body);
    let subj_proof = Proof::create(subject, &subj_sig);
    let room_proof = Proof::create(room, &room_sig);
    set_proof(&mut rec, ProofOrArray::array(vec![subj_proof, room_proof]));
    Ok(rec)
}

pub(crate) fn sign_record_triple<T, F>(
    mut rec: T,
    a: &Keypair,
    b: &Keypair,
    room: &Keypair,
    wrap: fn(T) -> record::Record,
    set_proof: F,
) -> Result<T>
where
    T: serde::Serialize + Clone,
    F: FnOnce(&mut T, ProofOrArray),
{
    let body = hash::body_for_signing(&wrap(rec.clone()))?;
    let a_sig = a.sign(&body);
    let b_sig = b.sign(&body);
    let room_sig = room.sign(&body);
    set_proof(&mut rec, ProofOrArray::array(vec![
        Proof::create(a, &a_sig),
        Proof::create(b, &b_sig),
        Proof::create(room, &room_sig),
    ]));
    Ok(rec)
}

src/room.rs
use crate::did::{DidKey, Keypair};
use crate::error::{Error, Result};
use crate::head::WorldHead;
use crate::record::*;
use crate::template::RoomTemplate;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};

fn short_hash(h: &str) -> String {
    h.chars().take(16).collect()
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RoomLog {
    pub world_id: DidKey,
    pub template: RoomTemplate,
    pub records: Vec<Record>,
    pub heads: Vec<WorldHead>,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Member {
    pub tier: String,
    pub is_ai: bool,
}

pub struct Room {
    pub world_id: DidKey,
    pub template: RoomTemplate,
    keypair: Keypair,
    records: Vec<Record>,
    members: HashMap<DidKey, Member>,
    heads: Vec<WorldHead>,
}

impl Room {
    pub fn create(
        template: RoomTemplate,
        room_kp: Keypair,
        creator_kp: &Keypair,
    ) -> Result<Self> {
        let world_id = room_kp.did();
        let creator_id = creator_kp.did();
        let mut members = HashMap::new();
        members.insert(
            creator_id.clone(),
            Member { tier: "terminal".into(), is_ai: false },
        );
        let mut room = Room {
            world_id: world_id.clone(),
            template: template.clone(),
            keypair: room_kp,
            records: Vec::new(),
            members,
            heads: Vec::new(),
        };
        let rec = RoomCreated::new(world_id, template, creator_id);
        let signed = crate::sign_record_dual(
            rec, creator_kp, &room.keypair, Record::RoomCreated, |r, p| r.proof = p,
        )?;
        room.records.push(Record::RoomCreated(signed));
        Ok(room)
    }

    pub fn log(&self) -> RoomLog {
        RoomLog {
            world_id: self.world_id.clone(),
            template: self.template.clone(),
            records: self.records.clone(),
            heads: self.heads.clone(),
        }
    }

    pub fn records(&self) -> &[Record] { &self.records }
    pub fn heads(&self) -> &[WorldHead] { &self.heads }
    pub fn last_hash(&self) -> Option<String> { self.records.last().and_then(|r| r.hash().ok()) }
    pub fn world_id(&self) -> &DidKey { &self.world_id }
    pub fn template(&self) -> &RoomTemplate { &self.template }
    pub fn members(&self) -> &HashMap<DidKey, Member> { &self.members }
    pub fn keypair(&self) -> &Keypair { &self.keypair }
    pub fn head_interval(&self) -> u64 { self.template.head_interval }

    pub fn is_redacted(&self, message_hash: &str) -> bool {
        let mut redact_hashes: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut resolved: HashSet<String> = HashSet::new();
        for rec in &self.records {
            match rec {
                Record::Redact(r) if r.target_hash == message_hash => {
                    if let Ok(h) = rec.hash() { redact_hashes.push(h); }
                }
                Record::HumanOverride(o) if o.action == "restore" => {
                    resolved.insert(o.overrides_hash.clone());
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        redact_hashes.iter().any(|h| !resolved.contains(h))
    }

    fn next_seq(&self) -> u64 { self.records.len() as u64 }

    fn should_emit_head(&self) -> bool {
        let n = self.template.head_interval;
        if n <= 1 { return false; }
        let appends = self.records.len().saturating_sub(1) as u64;
        appends > 0 && appends % n == 0
    }

    pub(crate) fn push(&mut self, rec: Record) -> Result<()> {
        self.records.push(rec);
        if self.should_emit_head() {
            let head = self.emit_head()?;
            self.heads.push(head);
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    fn refuse(&mut self, requester: DidKey, request_hash: String, reason: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let rec = Refusal::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            request_hash, requester, reason.into(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record(rec, &self.keypair, Record::Refusal, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        let out = Record::Refusal(signed);
        self.push(out.clone())?;
        Ok(out)
    }

    fn rate_ok(&self, author: &DidKey) -> bool {
        let window = self.template.rate_limit_window as usize;
        let cap = self.template.rate_limit_count as usize;
        let recent = self.records.iter().rev().take(window)
            .filter(|r| matches!(r, Record::Message(m) if &m.author_id == author))
            .count();
        recent < cap
    }

    fn inter_ai_pace_ok(&self, is_ai: bool) -> bool {
        if !is_ai || self.template.inter_ai_pace == 0 {
            return true;
        }
        let mut last_ai_idx: Option<usize> = None;
        for (i, rec) in self.records.iter().enumerate().rev() {
            if let Record::Message(m) = rec {
                let ai = self.members.get(&m.author_id).map(|x| x.is_ai).unwrap_or(false);
                if ai {
                    last_ai_idx = Some(i);
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        match last_ai_idx {
            None => true,
            Some(idx) => {
                let since = (self.records.len() - idx - 1) as u64;
                since >= self.template.inter_ai_pace
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn join(&mut self, subject_kp: &Keypair, tier: &str, is_ai: bool) -> Result<Record> {
        let subject = subject_kp.did();
        if self.members.contains_key(&subject) {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "already_member");
        }
        if tier_rank(tier).is_none() {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "unknown_tier");
        }
        if self.template.personal {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "personal_room");
        }
        if self.template.membership == "human_only" && is_ai {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "human_only_room");
        }
        if self.template.membership == "ai_only" && !is_ai {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "ai_only_room");
        }
        if self.template.join_policy == "invite" {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "invite_required");
        }
        if self.template.join_policy == "approval" {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "approval_required");
        }
        let rec = Join::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            subject.clone(), tier.into(), is_ai,
        );
        let signed = if self.template.head_interval <= 1 {
            crate::sign_record_dual(rec, subject_kp, &self.keypair, Record::Join, |r, p| r.proof = p)?
        } else {
            crate::sign_record(rec, subject_kp, Record::Join, |r, p| r.proof = p)?
        };
        self.members.insert(subject, Member { tier: tier.into(), is_ai });
        let out = Record::Join(signed);
        self.push(out.clone())?;
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn leave(&mut self, subject_kp: &Keypair) -> Result<Option<Record>> {
        let subject = subject_kp.did();
        if !self.members.contains_key(&subject) {
            return Ok(None);
        }
        let rec = Leave::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(), subject.clone(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record(rec, subject_kp, Record::Leave, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        self.members.remove(&subject);
        let out = Record::Leave(signed);
        self.push(out.clone())?;
        Ok(Some(out))
    }

    pub fn post(&mut self, author_kp: &Keypair, text: &str, kind: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let author = author_kp.did();
        let Some(m) = self.members.get(&author).cloned() else {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "not_a_member");
        };
        if tier_rank(&m.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("communicate").unwrap() {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "insufficient_tier");
        }
        if text.len() as u64 > self.template.max_msg_len {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "message_too_long");
        }
        if !self.rate_ok(&author) {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "rate_limit");
        }
        if !self.inter_ai_pace_ok(m.is_ai) {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "inter_ai_pace");
        }
        let rec = Message::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            author.clone(), text.into(), kind.into(),
        );
        let signed = if self.template.head_interval <= 1 {
            crate::sign_record_dual(rec, author_kp, &self.keypair, Record::Message, |r, p| r.proof = p)?
        } else {
            crate::sign_record(rec, author_kp, Record::Message, |r, p| r.proof = p)?
        };
        let out = Record::Message(signed);
        self.push(out.clone())?;
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn redact(
        &mut self,
        subject_kp: &Keypair,
        target_hash: &str,
        rule: &str,
        reason: &str,
    ) -> Result<Record> {
        let subject = subject_kp.did();
        let Some(m) = self.members.get(&subject).cloned() else {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "not_a_member");
        };
        let target = self.records.iter()
            .find(|r| r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(target_hash));
        let target_msg = match target {
            Some(Record::Message(msg)) => msg,
            _ => return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "target_not_found"),
        };
        if self.template.moderation == "none" {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "moderation_disabled");
        }
        let is_author = target_msg.author_id == subject;
        let is_mod = self.template.moderation == "moderator"
            && tier_rank(&m.tier).unwrap_or(0) >= tier_rank("control").unwrap();
        let actor_role = if is_author {
            "author"
        } else if is_mod {
            "moderator"
        } else {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "not_allowed");
        };
        if !self.template.redaction_rules.iter().any(|r| r == rule) {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "rule_not_allowed");
        }
        let rec = Redact::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            subject.clone(), target_hash.into(),
            actor_role.into(), rule.into(), reason.into(),
        );
        let signed = if self.template.head_interval <= 1 {
            crate::sign_record_dual(rec, subject_kp, &self.keypair, Record::Redact, |r, p| r.proof = p)?
        } else {
            crate::sign_record(rec, subject_kp, Record::Redact, |r, p| r.proof = p)?
        };
        let out = Record::Redact(signed);
        self.push(out.clone())?;
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn override_ai(&mut self, overrider_kp: &Keypair, overrides_hash: &str, reason: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let overrider = overrider_kp.did();
        let Some(m) = self.members.get(&overrider).cloned() else {
            return Err(Error::Policy("overrider is not a member".into()));
        };
        if m.is_ai {
            return Err(Error::Policy("only humans can override".into()));
        }
        if tier_rank(&m.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("control").unwrap() {
            return Err(Error::Policy("insufficient tier".into()));
        }
        let target = self.records.iter()
            .find(|r| r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(overrides_hash));
        let Some(target_rec) = target else {
            return Err(Error::Policy("target not found".into()));
        };
        let Some(author) = target_rec.author_id() else {
            return Err(Error::Policy("target has no author".into()));
        };
        let is_ai = self.members.get(author).map(|m| m.is_ai).unwrap_or(false);
        if !is_ai {
            return Err(Error::Policy("can only override AI records".into()));
        }
        if let Some(spec) = &self.template.moderator {
            if &spec.moderator_id == author && !spec.can_be_overridden {
                return Err(Error::Policy("this AI cannot be overridden".into()));
            }
        }
        let rec = HumanOverride::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            overrides_hash.into(), overrider.clone(), "none".into(), reason.into(),
        );
        let signed = if self.template.head_interval <= 1 {
            crate::sign_record_dual(rec, overrider_kp, &self.keypair, Record::HumanOverride, |r, p| r.proof = p)?
        } else {
            crate::sign_record(rec, overrider_kp, Record::HumanOverride, |r, p| r.proof = p)?
        };
        let out = Record::HumanOverride(signed);
        self.push(out.clone())?;
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn restore_incident(&mut self, restorer_kp: &Keypair, redact_hash: &str, reason: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let restorer = restorer_kp.did();
        let Some(m) = self.members.get(&restorer).cloned() else {
            return Err(Error::Policy("restorer is not a member".into()));
        };
        if m.is_ai {
            return Err(Error::Policy("only humans can restore".into()));
        }
        if tier_rank(&m.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("control").unwrap() {
            return Err(Error::Policy("insufficient tier".into()));
        }
        let target = self.records.iter()
            .find(|r| r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(redact_hash));
        match target {
            Some(Record::Redact(_)) => {}
            _ => return Err(Error::Policy("restore target must be a redact record".into())),
        }
        let rec = HumanOverride::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            redact_hash.into(), restorer.clone(), "restore".into(), reason.into(),
        );
        let signed = if self.template.head_interval <= 1 {
            crate::sign_record_dual(rec, restorer_kp, &self.keypair, Record::HumanOverride, |r, p| r.proof = p)?
        } else {
            crate::sign_record(rec, restorer_kp, Record::HumanOverride, |r, p| r.proof = p)?
        };
        let out = Record::HumanOverride(signed);
        self.push(out.clone())?;
        Ok(out)
    }

    /// Declare a moderator succession: the outgoing AI hands its role
    /// to the incoming. Triple-signed by outgoing, incoming, and the
    /// room. The outgoing must be a control+ member at this point.
    /// The shard_hash names the `KnowledgeShardExport` record in the
    /// outgoing's KnowledgeWorld; the crate cannot verify that
    /// cross-reference (it's a reader-side check when both logs are
    /// available), so the hash is taken on faith here.
    pub fn declare_succession(
        &mut self,
        outgoing_kp: &Keypair,
        incoming_kp: &Keypair,
        shard_hash: &str,
        reason: &str,
    ) -> Result<Record> {
        let outgoing_id = outgoing_kp.did();
        let incoming_id = incoming_kp.did();
        let Some(m) = self.members.get(&outgoing_id) else {
            return Err(Error::Policy("outgoing is not a member".into()));
        };
        if tier_rank(&m.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("control").unwrap() {
            return Err(Error::Policy("outgoing must be control or higher".into()));
        }
        if outgoing_id == incoming_id {
            return Err(Error::Policy("outgoing and incoming must differ".into()));
        }
        let rec = ModeratorSuccession::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            outgoing_id, incoming_id, shard_hash.into(), reason.into(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record_triple(
            rec, outgoing_kp, incoming_kp, &self.keypair,
            Record::ModeratorSuccession, |r, p| r.proof = p,
        )?;
        let out = Record::ModeratorSuccession(signed);
        self.push(out.clone())?;
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn visible_log(&self) -> Vec<serde_json::Value> {
        use serde_json::json;

        let hashes: Vec<String> = self.records.iter()
            .map(|r| r.hash().unwrap_or_default())
            .collect();
        let mut hash_to_idx: HashMap<String, usize> = HashMap::new();
        for (i, h) in hashes.iter().enumerate() {
            hash_to_idx.insert(h.clone(), i);
        }

        let mut redactions_by_target: HashMap<usize, Vec<usize>> = HashMap::new();
        let mut resolved_redacts: HashSet<usize> = HashSet::new();
        for (i, rec) in self.records.iter().enumerate() {
            match rec {
                Record::Redact(r) => {
                    if let Some(&target_idx) = hash_to_idx.get(&r.target_hash) {
                        redactions_by_target.entry(target_idx).or_default().push(i);
                    }
                }
                Record::HumanOverride(o) if o.action == "restore" => {
                    if let Some(&redact_idx) = hash_to_idx.get(&o.overrides_hash) {
                        resolved_redacts.insert(redact_idx);
                    }
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }

        let mut out = Vec::new();
        for (i, rec) in self.records.iter().enumerate() {
            match rec {
                Record::Message(m) => {
                    let mut still_redacted: Option<usize> = None;
                    let mut restored: Vec<usize> = Vec::new();
                    if let Some(redact_idxs) = redactions_by_target.get(&i) {
                        for &ri in redact_idxs {
                            if resolved_redacts.contains(&ri) {
                                restored.push(ri);
                            } else if still_redacted.is_none() {
                                still_redacted = Some(ri);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if let Some(ri) = still_redacted {
                        if let Record::Redact(r) = &self.records[ri] {
                            out.push(json!({
                                "seq": m.seq, "type": "message", "kind": m.kind,
                                "author": m.author_id.as_str(),
                                "text": "[removed]",
                                "redacted": true,
                                "reason": r.reason,
                                "rule": r.rule,
                                "incident": short_hash(&hashes[ri]),
                                "ts": m.ts,
                            }));
                        }
                    } else {
                        let mut obj = json!({
                            "seq": m.seq, "type": "message", "kind": m.kind,
                            "author": m.author_id.as_str(),
                            "text": m.text,
                            "redacted": false,
                            "ts": m.ts,
                        });
                        if !restored.is_empty() {
                            let notes: Vec<serde_json::Value> = restored.iter().map(|&ri| {
                                if let Record::Redact(r) = &self.records[ri] {
                                    json!({"incident": short_hash(&hashes[ri]), "reason": r.reason, "rule": r.rule})
                                } else { json!({}) }
                            }).collect();
                            obj["restored_incidents"] = json!(notes);
                        }
                        out.push(obj);
                    }
                }
                Record::Redact(r) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": r.seq, "type": "redact", "by": r.subject_id.as_str(), "reason": r.reason, "target": r.target_hash, "rule": r.rule, "actor_role": r.actor_role, "incident": short_hash(&hashes[i])}));
                }
                Record::Join(j) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": j.seq, "type": "join", "subject": j.subject_id.as_str(), "tier": j.tier, "is_ai": j.is_ai}));
                }
                Record::Leave(l) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": l.seq, "type": "leave", "subject": l.subject_id.as_str()}));
                }
                Record::HumanOverride(o) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": o.seq, "type": "human_override", "by": o.overridden_by.as_str(), "overrides": o.overrides_hash, "action": o.action, "reason": o.reason}));
                }
                Record::Refusal(r) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": r.seq, "type": "refused", "requester": r.requester_id.as_str(), "reason": r.reason}));
                }
                Record::RoomCreated(_) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": 0, "type": "created"}));
                }
                Record::ModeratorSuccession(s) => {
                    out.push(json!({
                        "seq": s.seq, "type": "moderator_succession",
                        "outgoing": s.outgoing_id.as_str(),
                        "incoming": s.incoming_id.as_str(),
                        "reason": s.reason,
                    }));
                }
                Record::KnowledgeShardExport(_) | Record::PortLanding(_) | Record::Bookmark(_) | Record::Presence(_) => {}
            }
        }
        out
    }
}

src/head.rs
//! Signed heads.
//!
//! A head is a small, signed commitment to a log's prefix: how
//! many entries it holds, what the last one hashes to, and when
//! (in the room's sense of "when"). It is a *published artifact*,
//! not a record in the log. A room in `head_interval > 1` mode
//! emits a head every N appended records; peers can verify a
//! head alone and know the log's prefix is fixed.

use crate::did::DidKey;
use crate::error::{Error, Result};
use crate::hash;
use crate::proof::{Proof, ProofOrArray};
use crate::room::{Room, RoomLog};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

pub const HEAD_FORMAT_V1: &str = "open-worlds/log-head/v1";

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct WorldHead {
    pub format: String,
    pub world: DidKey,
    pub length: u64,
    pub last: String,
    pub tick: u64,
    pub proof: ProofOrArray,
}

impl WorldHead {
    pub fn hash(&self) -> Result<String> {
        hash::hash_of(self)
    }
}

impl RoomLog {
    /// Verify a head against a published `RoomLog`.
    /// The log must be at least as long as the head; the head's
    /// prefix must be intact and its signature valid under `self.world_id`.
    pub fn verify_head(&self, head: &WorldHead) -> Result<()> {
        if head.format != HEAD_FORMAT_V1 {
            return Err(Error::Verification(format!(
                "head: bad format {:?}",
                head.format
            )));
        }
        if head.world != self.world_id {
            return Err(Error::Verification("head: world mismatch".into()));
        }
        if head.length as usize > self.records.len() {
            return Err(Error::Verification(format!(
                "head: length {} exceeds log length {}",
                head.length,
                self.records.len()
            )));
        }
        if head.length == 0 {
            return Err(Error::Verification("head: length 0".into()));
        }
        if head.length != head.tick {
            return Err(Error::Verification(format!(
                "head: tick {} != length {}",
                head.tick, head.length
            )));
        }
        let prefix = &self.records[..head.length as usize];
        let last_hash = prefix[prefix.len() - 1].hash()?;
        if last_hash != head.last {
            return Err(Error::Verification("head: last mismatch".into()));
        }
        let body = hash::body_for_signing(head)?;
        let proof = head
            .proof
            .find_by_method(&self.world_id)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification("head: missing room proof".into()))?;
        proof.verify(&body)
    }
}

impl Room {
    /// Emit a head for the room's current state. Does NOT append
    /// to the room's log; the head is a published artifact.
    pub fn emit_head(&self) -> Result<WorldHead> {
        let last_rec = self
            .records()
            .last()
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::Policy("cannot emit head for empty room".into()))?;
        let last_hash = last_rec.hash()?;
        let length = self.records().len() as u64;
        let mut head = WorldHead {
            format: HEAD_FORMAT_V1.into(),
            world: self.world_id().clone(),
            length,
            last: last_hash,
            tick: length,
            proof: ProofOrArray::placeholder(),
        };
        let body = hash::body_for_signing(&head)?;
        let sig = self.keypair().sign(&body);
        head.proof = ProofOrArray::single(Proof::create(self.keypair(), &sig));
        Ok(head)
    }

    /// Verify a head against the room it claims to be about.
    pub fn verify_head(&self, head: &WorldHead) -> Result<()> {
        self.log().verify_head(head)
    }
}

src/verify.rs
use crate::did::DidKey;
use crate::error::{Error, Result};
use crate::hash;
use crate::record::*;
use crate::room::{Member, Room};
pub use crate::room::RoomLog;
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};

fn looks_like_hash(s: &str) -> bool {
    s.len() == 64 && s.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_hexdigit())
}

pub fn verify_room(room: &Room) -> Result<()> {
    verify_log(&room.log())
}

pub fn verify_log(log: &RoomLog) -> Result<()> {
    let (world_id, creator_id, template, per_record_mode) = match log.records.first() {
        Some(Record::RoomCreated(rc)) => {
            if rc.world_id != log.world_id {
                return Err(Error::Verification(
                    "room_created world_id does not match log world_id".into(),
                ));
            }
            if rc.template.hash()? != log.template.hash()? {
                return Err(Error::Verification(
                    "room_created template does not match log template".into(),
                ));
            }
            (
                &rc.world_id,
                rc.creator_id.clone(),
                &rc.template,
                rc.template.head_interval <= 1,
            )
        }
        _ => return Err(Error::Chain("log must start with room_created".into())),
    };

    let mut prev: Option<String> = None;
    let mut members: HashMap<DidKey, Member> = HashMap::new();
    let mut redacted: HashSet<String> = HashSet::new();

    for (i, rec) in log.records.iter().enumerate() {
        match rec {
            Record::RoomCreated(_) => {
                if i != 0 {
                    return Err(Error::Chain(format!("record {i}: room_created not at start")));
                }
            }
            _ => {
                if rec.prev() != prev.as_ref() {
                    return Err(Error::Chain(format!("record {i}: prev mismatch")));
                }
            }
        }

        if rec.world_id() != world_id {
            return Err(Error::Verification(format!(
                "record {i}: world_id mismatch"
            )));
        }

        let body = hash::body_for_signing(rec)?;
        let proofs = rec.proof();

        let requires_room_proof = match rec {
            Record::RoomCreated(_) => true,
            Record::Refusal(_) => true,
            Record::KnowledgeShardExport(_) => true,
            Record::ModeratorSuccession(_) => true,
            Record::Leave(_) => false,
            _ => per_record_mode,
        };
        if requires_room_proof {
            let rp = proofs.find_by_method(world_id).ok_or_else(|| {
                Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing room proof"))
            })?;
            rp.verify(&body)?;
        }

        match rec {
            Record::RoomCreated(r) => {
                let creator_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&r.creator_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing creator proof")))?;
                creator_proof.verify(&body)?;
                members.insert(r.creator_id.clone(), Member { tier: "terminal".into(), is_ai: false });
            }
            Record::Message(m) => {
                let Some(mem) = members.get(&m.author_id) else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: author not a member")));
                };
                if tier_rank(&mem.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("communicate").unwrap() {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: author tier insufficient")));
                }
                let author_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&m.author_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing author proof")))?;
                author_proof.verify(&body)?;
            }
            Record::Join(j) => {
                let subject_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&j.subject_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing subject proof")))?;
                subject_proof.verify(&body)?;
                members.insert(j.subject_id.clone(), Member { tier: j.tier.clone(), is_ai: j.is_ai });
            }
            Record::Leave(l) => {
                let subject_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&l.subject_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing subject proof")))?;
                subject_proof.verify(&body)?;
                members.remove(&l.subject_id);
            }
            Record::Redact(r) => {
                let subject_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&r.subject_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing subject proof")))?;
                subject_proof.verify(&body)?;
                let Some(mem) = members.get(&r.subject_id) else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: redactor not a member")));
                };
                let target = log.records.iter()
                    .find(|x| x.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(&r.target_hash));
                let target_msg = match target {
                    Some(Record::Message(m)) => m,
                    _ => return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: redact target not a message"))),
                };
                if template.moderation == "none" {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: moderation disabled")));
                }
                let is_author = target_msg.author_id == r.subject_id;
                let is_mod = template.moderation == "moderator"
                    && tier_rank(&mem.tier).unwrap_or(0) >= tier_rank("control").unwrap();
                let role_ok = match r.actor_role.as_str() {
                    "author" => is_author,
                    "moderator" => is_mod,
                    _ => false,
                };
                if !role_ok {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: actor_role {:?} not permitted", r.actor_role)));
                }
                if !template.redaction_rules.iter().any(|x| x == &r.rule) {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: rule {:?} not allowed", r.rule)));
                }
                redacted.insert(r.target_hash.clone());
            }
            Record::HumanOverride(o) => {
                let overrider_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&o.overridden_by)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing overrider proof")))?;
                overrider_proof.verify(&body)?;
                let Some(mem) = members.get(&o.overridden_by) else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: overrider not a member")));
                };
                if mem.is_ai {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: only humans can override")));
                }
                if tier_rank(&mem.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("control").unwrap() {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: overrider tier insufficient")));
                }
                let target = log.records.iter()
                    .find(|x| x.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(&o.overrides_hash));
                let Some(target) = target else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: override target not found")));
                };
                match o.action.as_str() {
                    "none" => {
                        let Some(author) = target.author_id() else {
                            return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: override 'none' target has no author")));
                        };
                        let target_is_ai = members.get(author).map(|m| m.is_ai).unwrap_or(false);
                        if !target_is_ai {
                            return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: override 'none' target must be AI-authored")));
                        }
                    }
                    "restore" => {
                        if !matches!(target, Record::Redact(_)) {
                            return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: restore target must be a redact record")));
                        }
                    }
                    other => {
                        return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: unknown override action {other:?}")));
                    }
                }
            }
            Record::Refusal(_) => {}
            Record::PortLanding(p) => {
                if !template.personal {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: port_landing only in personal rooms")));
                }
                crate::transcript::verify_transcript(&p.transcript)
                    .map_err(|e| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: transcript: {e}")))?;
                if p.intent != "journal" && p.intent != "room" {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: unknown port intent")));
                }
                if p.intent == "room" && p.target_room.is_none() {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: intent 'room' without target_room")));
                }
            }
            Record::Bookmark(_) => {
                if !template.personal {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: bookmark only in personal rooms")));
                }
            }
            Record::Presence(p) => {
                if !template.personal {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: presence only in personal rooms")));
                }
                if p.subject_id != creator_id {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: presence subject must be creator")));
                }
            }
            Record::KnowledgeShardExport(k) => {
                if !template.personal {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: knowledge_shard_export only in personal rooms")));
                }
                if k.entry_hashes.iter().any(|h| !looks_like_hash(h)) {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: shard entry hash not a 64-hex string")));
                }
                // Room proof already verified above.
            }
            Record::ModeratorSuccession(s) => {
                // Room proof verified above. Outgoing + incoming also sign.
                let outgoing_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&s.outgoing_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing outgoing proof")))?;
                outgoing_proof.verify(&body)?;
                let incoming_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&s.incoming_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing incoming proof")))?;
                incoming_proof.verify(&body)?;
                // Outgoing must be a control+ member at this point.
                let Some(mem) = members.get(&s.outgoing_id) else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: outgoing not a member")));
                };
                if tier_rank(&mem.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("control").unwrap() {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: outgoing tier insufficient")));
                }
                if s.outgoing_id == s.incoming_id {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: outgoing == incoming")));
                }
                if !looks_like_hash(&s.knowledge_shard_hash) {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: knowledge_shard_hash not a 64-hex string")));
                }
            }
        }

        prev = Some(rec.hash()?);
    }

    // Heads
    let mut last_length: u64 = 0;
    for (i, head) in log.heads.iter().enumerate() {
        log.verify_head(head).map_err(|e| Error::Verification(format!("head {i}: {e}")))?;
        if head.length <= last_length {
            return Err(Error::Verification(format!("head {i}: length not increasing")));
        }
        last_length = head.length;
    }
    if per_record_mode && !log.heads.is_empty() {
        return Err(Error::Verification("per-record mode should not emit heads".into()));
    }
    if !per_record_mode {
        let n = template.head_interval;
        let appends = log.records.len().saturating_sub(1) as u64;
        let expected = if n > 1 { appends / n } else { 0 };
        if expected > 0 && log.heads.len() as u64 != expected {
            return Err(Error::Verification(format!(
                "head count: expected {} for {} appends at interval {}, got {}",
                expected, appends, n, log.heads.len()
            )));
        }
    }

    if template.personal {
        for rec in &log.records {
            if let Record::Join(j) = rec {
                if j.subject_id != creator_id {
                    return Err(Error::Policy("personal room: non-creator joined".into()));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    Ok(())
}

pub fn verify_chain(room: &Room) -> Result<()> {
    verify_log_chain(&room.log())
}

pub fn verify_log_chain(log: &RoomLog) -> Result<()> {
    let mut prev: Option<String> = None;
    for (i, rec) in log.records.iter().enumerate() {
        match rec {
            Record::RoomCreated(_) if i != 0 => {
                return Err(Error::Chain("room_created not at start".into()));
            }
            Record::RoomCreated(_) => {}
            _ => {
                if rec.prev() != prev.as_ref() {
                    return Err(Error::Chain(format!("record {i}: prev mismatch")));
                }
            }
        }
        prev = Some(rec.hash()?);
    }
    Ok(())
}

tests/outside.rs
use chat_world::error::Result;
use chat_world::home::HomeRoom;
use chat_world::transcript::{sign_transcript, TranscriptEntry};
use chat_world::verify::verify_log;
use chat_world::{Keypair, Room, RoomLog, RoomTemplate};

fn make_room() -> Room {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let template = RoomTemplate::chat("t", "test");
    Room::create(template, room_kp, &alice).unwrap()
}

fn make_head_mode_room(interval: u64) -> Room {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let mut template = RoomTemplate::chat("t", "test");
    template.head_interval = interval;
    Room::create(template, room_kp, &alice).unwrap()
}

#[test]
fn honest_room_log_roundtrips_json_and_verifies() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "hello from bob", "normal")?;
    let msg_hash = msg.hash()?;
    room.redact(&bob, &msg_hash, "author_request", "typo")?;

    let json_text = serde_json::to_string(&room.log())
        .expect("serialize RoomLog to JSON");
    let parsed: RoomLog = serde_json::from_str(&json_text)
        .expect("deserialize RoomLog from JSON");

    verify_log(&parsed)
}

#[test]
fn honest_home_log_roundtrips_json_and_verifies() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let home_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let mut home = HomeRoom::create(alice.clone(), home_kp, "alice home")?;
    home.journal("first journal entry")?;
    home.draft("draft thought", None)?;
    let entries = vec![TranscriptEntry {
        speaker: "user".into(),
        ts: chat_world::now(),
        text: "ported line".into(),
    }];
    let t = sign_transcript("external-app", entries, &alice)?;
    home.port(&t, "journal", None)?;

    let json_text = serde_json::to_string(&home.room().log())
        .expect("serialize home RoomLog to JSON");
    let parsed: RoomLog = serde_json::from_str(&json_text)
        .expect("deserialize home RoomLog from JSON");

    verify_log(&parsed)
}

#[test]
fn honest_head_mode_room_log_roundtrips_json_and_verifies() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_head_mode_room(2);
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.post(&bob, "first", "normal")?;
    room.post(&bob, "second", "normal")?;
    room.post(&bob, "third", "normal")?;
    assert_eq!(room.heads().len(), 2);

    let json_text = serde_json::to_string(&room.log())
        .expect("serialize head-mode RoomLog to JSON");
    let parsed: RoomLog = serde_json::from_str(&json_text)
        .expect("deserialize head-mode RoomLog from JSON");

    verify_log(&parsed)
}

Expected: 44 tests pass (41 in tests/integration.rs and 3 in tests/outside.rs).


::➡️


STEP 2 writes tests/attack.rs to probe verify_log against forged, reordered, and policy-violating RoomLog instances. Every test constructs or mutates a RoomLog outside Room's methods and asserts verify_log(&log).is_err().
Attack Inventory and Expected Outcomes
Every test below is expected to PASS (verify_log refuses the forged log), except for two subtle vulnerabilities I found in verify_log as it stands today, which are expected to FAIL (verify_log accepts what it should refuse) and will be repaired in Step 3:
 * Proofs
   * attack_proofs_missing_room_proof_in_per_record_mode: PASS — requires_room_proof is true in per-record mode (head_interval <= 1), so a message carrying only its author's proof is refused.
   * attack_proofs_moved_from_one_record_to_another: PASS — moving the ProofOrArray from one message to another with different text fails signature verification over body_for_signing.
   * attack_proofs_signed_by_key_that_never_joined: PASS — a message signed by a non-member (even if countersigned by the room key) is refused by the membership table check.
   * attack_proofs_signed_by_key_that_has_left: PASS — once a Leave record removes the member from members, a subsequent message signed by that key is refused.
 * Order
   * attack_order_two_records_swapped: PASS — swapping two records breaks rec.prev() != prev.as_ref().
   * attack_order_record_dropped_from_middle: PASS — dropping a record from the middle breaks prev on the following record.
   * attack_order_record_repeated: PASS — repeating a record at the end has rec.prev() pointing to the predecessor of the first copy rather than the hash of the first copy itself.
   * attack_order_tail_of_another_rooms_log_joined_on: PASS — even if prev and seq are re-signed on a record from another room, its world_id does not match.
 * Content
   * attack_content_message_text_changed_after_signing: PASS — mutating m.text after signing invalidates both the author and room signatures.
   * attack_content_record_type_changed: PASS — because sign_record signs the serialized Record enum ("type" tag included), re-wrapping a signed Leave's fields and proofs as a Join changes "type" in body_for_signing and invalidates the signature.
   * attack_content_record_moved_to_another_world_id: PASS — changing world_id on a record invalidates the signature and fails the world_id check.
 * Rules
   * attack_rules_redaction_by_non_author_non_moderator: PASS — a Redact record built by hand and signed by a communicate-tier non-author (plus the room key) is caught by role_ok in verify_log.
   * attack_rules_redaction_naming_disallowed_rule: PASS — a Redact record naming a rule not in template.redaction_rules is refused.
   * attack_rules_second_member_in_personal_room: PASS — a Join record for a second user in a personal: true room (even if dual-signed by the room key) is refused by the personal room check at the end of verify_log.
   * attack_rules_rate_limit_bypassed_in_forged_log: EXPECTED TO FAIL (vulnerability found!) — Room::post enforces rate_limit_count / rate_limit_window, max_msg_len, and inter_ai_pace, and Room::join enforces membership (human_only / ai_only), join_policy (invite / approval), and personal during live room calls, but verify_log does not replay rate limits, max_msg_len, inter_ai_pace, or membership (human_only/ai_only) on Record::Message / Record::Join! A forged log in head_interval > 1 mode (or with a colluding room key) can stuff 100 messages in a window of 5 when rate_limit_count == 2, and verify_log will ACCEPT it.
   * attack_rules_human_only_room_with_forged_ai_join: EXPECTED TO FAIL (vulnerability found!) — if a Record::Join with is_ai: true is placed into a human_only room's log (for instance in head mode where Join needs only the subject's signature before the next head, or with the room key), verify_log never checks template.membership on Record::Join and will ACCEPT it.
 * Template
   * attack_template_log_template_differs_from_room_created: PASS — verify_log checks rc.template.hash()? != log.template.hash()? and refuses.
 * Heads
   * attack_heads_head_from_another_room: PASS — verify_head checks head.world != self.world_id.
   * attack_heads_head_length_past_end_of_log: PASS — verify_head checks head.length as usize > self.records.len().
   * attack_heads_old_head_given_again_as_newest: PASS — verify_log checks head.length <= last_length.
   * attack_heads_head_in_per_record_mode_room: PASS — verify_log checks if per_record_mode && !log.heads.is_empty().
   * attack_heads_unanchored_trailing_forged_record_in_head_mode: EXPECTED TO FAIL (vulnerability found!) — in head_interval = 2 mode, after 2 appends (length 3), 1 head is emitted covering length 3. If an attacker appends a 3rd record (length = 4, so appends = 3, expected = 3 / 2 = 1 head), verify_log sees heads.len() == 1 == expected, and because per_record_mode is false, individual records do not require a room proof! That means an attacker can append an un-countersigned trailing record after the last head (as long as appends % head_interval != 0), and verify_log will ACCEPT a log whose tail is not covered by any room signature.
tests/attack.rs
use chat_world::error::Result;
use chat_world::hash::body_for_signing;
use chat_world::head::{WorldHead, HEAD_FORMAT_V1};
use chat_world::record::*;
use chat_world::verify::verify_log;
use chat_world::{Keypair, Proof, ProofOrArray, Room, RoomLog, RoomTemplate};

fn sign_single(rec: &Record, signer: &Keypair) -> ProofOrArray {
    let body = body_for_signing(rec).unwrap();
    let sig = signer.sign(&body);
    ProofOrArray::Single(Proof::create(signer, &sig))
}

fn sign_dual(rec: &Record, subj: &Keypair, room_kp: &Keypair) -> ProofOrArray {
    let body = body_for_signing(rec).unwrap();
    let s1 = subj.sign(&body);
    let s2 = room_kp.sign(&body);
    ProofOrArray::array(vec![
        Proof::create(subj, &s1),
        Proof::create(room_kp, &s2),
    ])
}

// =====================================================================
// 1. PROOFS
// =====================================================================

#[test]
fn attack_proofs_missing_room_proof_in_per_record_mode() -> Result<()> {
    // Expected: PASS (verify_log refuses a message with only author proof in per-record mode)
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let mut room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "test"), room_kp, &alice)?;
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;

    let mut log = room.log();
    let prev = log.records.last().unwrap().hash()?;
    let mut msg = Message::new(
        log.world_id.clone(),
        log.records.len() as u64,
        Some(prev),
        bob.did(),
        "forged without room sig".into(),
        "normal".into(),
    );
    let wrapped = Record::Message(msg.clone());
    msg.proof = sign_single(&wrapped, &bob);
    log.records.push(Record::Message(msg));

    assert!(verify_log(&log).is_err());
    Ok(())
}

    let room_kp = Keypair::generate();

    room.post(&bob, "first message", "normal")?;
    room.post(&bob, "second message", "normal")?;

        m2.proof = first_proof;
    }

    assert!(verify_log(&log).is_err());
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_content_record_type_changed() -> Result<()> {
    // Expected: PASS (verify_log refuses when a signed Leave is re-tagged as another record type)
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let mut room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "test"), room_kp.clone(), &alice)?;
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.leave(&bob)?;

    let mut log = room.log();
    let leave_rec = log.records.pop().unwrap();
    if let Record::Leave(l) = leave_rec {
        // Attempt to turn the Leave into a Bookmark carrying the same proofs
        let bm = Bookmark {
            world_id: l.world_id,
            seq: l.seq,
            prev: l.prev,
            room_id: l.subject_id,
            note: "".into(),
            ts: l.ts,
            proof: l.proof,
        };
        log.records.push(Record::Bookmark(bm));
    }

    assert!(verify_log(&log).is_err());
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_rules_redaction_by_non_author_non_moderator() -> Result<()> {
    // Expected: PASS (verify_log refuses a hand-built Redact signed by a non-author communicate member)
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let mallory = Keypair::generate();
    let room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let mut room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "test"), room_kp.clone(), &alice)?;
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.join(&mallory, "communicate", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "bob's message", "normal")?;
    let msg_hash = msg.hash()?;

    let mut log = room.log();
    let prev = log.records.last().unwrap().hash()?;
    let mut red = Redact::new(
        log.world_id.clone(),
        log.records.len() as u64,
        Some(prev),
        mallory.did(),
        msg_hash,
        "moderator".into(),
        "spam".into(),
        "forged redact".into(),
    );
    let wrapped = Record::Redact(red.clone());
    red.proof = sign_dual(&wrapped, &mallory, &room_kp);
    log.records.push(Record::Redact(red));

    assert!(verify_log(&log).is_err());
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_rules_rate_limit_bypassed_in_forged_log() -> Result<()> {
    // Expected: FAIL on current code (verify_log does not replay rate_limit_count/window!)
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let mut template = RoomTemplate::chat("t", "test");
    template.rate_limit_count = 2;
    template.rate_limit_window = 5;
    let mut room = Room::create(template, room_kp.clone(), &alice)?;
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.post(&bob, "one", "normal")?;
    room.post(&bob, "two", "normal")?;

    // Build a 3rd message by hand, bypassing Room::post's rate_ok check
    let mut log = room.log();
    let prev = log.records.last().unwrap().hash()?;
    let mut msg = Message::new(
        log.world_id.clone(),
        log.records.len() as u64,
        Some(prev),
        bob.did(),
        "three (exceeds rate limit)".into(),
        "normal".into(),
    );
    let wrapped = Record::Message(msg.clone());
    msg.proof = sign_dual(&wrapped, &bob, &room_kp);
    log.records.push(Record::Message(msg));

    assert!(verify_log(&log).is_err());
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_heads_head_from_another_room() -> Result<()> {
    // Expected: PASS (verify_log refuses a head signed by another room)
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let room_a_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let room_b_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let mut t_a = RoomTemplate::chat("t", "A");
    t_a.head_interval = 2;
    let mut t_b = RoomTemplate::chat("t", "B");
    t_b.head_interval = 2;

    let mut room_a = Room::create(t_a, room_a_kp, &alice)?;
    let mut room_b = Room::create(t_b, room_b_kp, &alice)?;
    room_a.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room_a.post(&bob, "msg", "normal")?;
    room_b.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room_b.post(&bob, "msg", "normal")?;

    let mut log_a = room_a.log();
    log_a.heads[0] = room_b.log().heads[0].clone();

    assert!(verify_log(&log_a).is_err());
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_heads_head_length_past_end_of_log() -> Result<()> {
    // Expected: PASS (verify_log refuses a head whose length exceeds records.len())
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let mut template = RoomTemplate::chat("t", "test");
    template.head_interval = 2;
    let mut room = Room::create(template, room_kp.clone(), &alice)?;
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.post(&bob, "msg", "normal")?;

    let mut log = room.log();
    let last_hash = log.records.last().unwrap().hash()?;
    let mut bad_head = WorldHead {
        format: HEAD_FORMAT_V1.into(),
        world: log.world_id.clone(),
        length: 99,
        last: last_hash,
        tick: 99,
        proof: ProofOrArray::placeholder(),
    };
    let body = body_for_signing(&bad_head)?;
    let sig = room_kp.sign(&body);
    bad_head.proof = ProofOrArray::single(Proof::create(&room_kp, &sig));
    log.heads[0] = bad_head;

    assert!(verify_log(&log).is_err());
    Ok(())
}

#[test]

    template.head_interval = 2;

    log.heads[1] = head0;

}
