END OF FILE
To prevent as_text from ever rendering an unchecked DoorList to a mind, have draw and check return a verified wrapper type CheckedDoorList(DoorList) whose inner field is private to src/doors.rs, and change as_text to take &CheckedDoorList (or have draw_as_text(log, rooms) -> Result<String> run draw first), so the Rust type system makes it impossible to format a DoorList that did not pass through draw or check.
Below is the inventory of the 25 attack tests in tests/doors_attack.rs (the 22 specified in the brief plus 3 additional attacks), with what each test does, whether it is expected to PASS or FAIL, and what a reader would need to hold to catch the ones that cannot be refused from the logs alone:
 * 1. attack_01_door_added_for_unlisted_room — PASS: check recomputes draw and refuses when list.doors.len() exceeds the registrations in log.
 * 2. attack_02_door_left_out — PASS: check refuses when a registered door is omitted from list.doors.
 * 3. attack_03_two_doors_swapped — PASS: check compares doors in exact registration order and refuses when two doors are swapped.
 * 4. attack_04_door_fields_changed_in_turn — PASS: mutating topic, room_id, template_hash, registration_seq, or registration_hash on a door is refused by check in all five cases.
 * 5. attack_05_law_changed_invite_shown_as_open — PASS: changing law.join_policy from "invite" to "open" in the DoorList is refused by check.
 * 6. attack_06_law_taken_away_when_room_log_held — PASS: setting door.law = None when the reader holds the room's log is refused by check.
 * 7. attack_07_law_and_count_given_when_room_log_not_held — PASS: supplying Some(law) or Some(count) in list when the reader does not hold the room's log (rooms = &[]) is refused by check.
 * 8. attack_08_count_forgeries_in_turn — PASS: changing count.members, changing count.at_record, adding Some(count) to a count_hidden: true room, and setting count = None on a count_hidden: false room are each refused by check.
 * 9. attack_09_list_header_fields_changed_in_turn — PASS: mutating drawn_from, log_head, or commons_id on DoorList is refused by check in all three cases.
 * 10. attack_10_list_from_another_commons_checked_against_this_one — PASS: a DoorList honestly drawn from a second Commons listing the same rooms is refused by check against the first Commons (commons_id and registration_hash differ).
 * 11. attack_11_commons_log_registration_dropped_from_middle — PASS: draw runs verify_commons_log, which refuses when a middle registration is dropped (seq and prev mismatch).
 * 12. attack_12_room_log_given_under_another_rooms_registration — PASS: rewriting a RoomLog's world_id to match another room's registration is refused by draw via verify_registration (rc.world_id != log.world_id).
 * 13. attack_13_room_log_template_count_hidden_changed_each_way — PASS: flipping room_log.template.count_hidden (false -> true or true -> false) in the RoomLog is refused by draw via verify_log (rc.template.hash() != log.template.hash()).
 * 14. attack_14_room_log_leave_dropped_from_middle — PASS: dropping a Leave record from the middle of a RoomLog to inflate the count is refused by draw via verify_log (seq and prev mismatch).
 * 15. attack_15_room_log_join_added_at_end_without_subject_signature — PASS: appending a Join signed only by the room key (without the subject's signature) is refused by draw via verify_log.
 * 16. attack_16_room_log_same_key_joins_twice_properly_signed — PASS: appending a second Join for a key already in the room (dual-signed by the subject and the room) is refused by draw via verify_log (already_member).
 * 17. attack_17_room_log_cut_short_just_before_leave — EXPECTED TO FAIL (accepted by draw!): in per-record mode (head_interval == 1), truncating a RoomLog just before a Leave record still leaves a valid prefix where every record carries a room signature, so draw accepts the truncated log and produces the older, larger member count (members: 3, at_record: 3 instead of members: 2, at_record: 4). What a reader needs to hold to catch it: a signed WorldHead (or peer checkpoint) from the room committing to the true current length (length: 4), so any RoomLog shorter than the latest known WorldHead is refused as stale.
 * 18. attack_18_json_door_room_id_not_a_did_key — PASS: deserializing a DoorList from JSON where room_id is "not a did" is refused by DidKey::deserialize.
 * 19. attack_19_json_door_with_extra_members_field_leaking_ids — EXPECTED TO FAIL (accepted by serde_json::from_value!): because Door, DoorLaw, DoorCount, and DoorList in src/doors.rs do not have #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)], serde_json silently ignores an injected "members": ["did:key:...", "did:key:..."] field in JSON and succeeds in parsing the DoorList, allowing a JSON payload carrying hidden personal IDs to pass serde_json::from_str and check!
 * 20. attack_20_text_welcome_with_line_feed_and_fake_door_line — PASS: a welcome containing "\n2. topic \"Fake\"\n" is escaped by quote_ascii (\u000a), so as_text has the exact same line count as an honest welcome without newlines.
 * 21. attack_21_text_law_join_policy_with_line_feed — PASS: a DoorLaw whose join_policy (or membership, moderation, mode) contains a newline is escaped by quote_ascii, so as_text has the exact same line count as an honest law line.
 * 22. attack_22_sybil_thirty_keys_from_one_holder_outranks_five_people — EXPECTED TO FAIL (accepted by draw and ranked!): one person generates 30 fresh keypairs, joins an open room 30 times, and pushes that room to #1 in ranked(&list) (members: 31) above a room of 5 distinct people (members: 5). The test asserts that a room inflated by 30 keys from one holder should not outrank the 5-person room (ranked(&list)[0].room_id == five_person_room.world_id()), so the test fails because draw counts distinct DidKeys and cannot tell whether 30 keys belong to one holder. What a reader (or room law) would need to hold to catch it: either a room law requiring RuntimeAttestation / proof-of-personhood credentials on Join, or cross-checking against a RuntimeAttestation showing that those 30 keys share a single runtime/holder.
 * 23. attack_23_room_template_changed_after_registration_via_recreated_room_with_same_key — PASS: if a room's keeper creates a new RoomLog using the same room_kp and alice keypair but with a changed template (e.g. moderation: "none" instead of "moderator"), verify_log passes on the new RoomLog alone, but draw refuses it via verify_registration because reg.template_hash and reg.room_seed_hash in CommonsLog do not match the re-seeded RoomLog.
 * 24. attack_24_room_log_leave_for_non_member_does_not_underflow_or_corrupt_count — EXPECTED TO FAIL (accepted by verify_log and draw!): in src/verify.rs, Record::Leave(l) verifies l.subject_id's signature and calls members.remove(&l.subject_id), but never checks whether l.subject_id was actually a member of the room! A non-member can sign and append a Record::Leave (which never requires a room proof, even in per-record mode!) to any RoomLog, and draw accepts the tampered log and advances at_record without error!
 * 25. attack_25_stale_registration_topic_differs_from_room_template_topic_when_none_was_intended — EXPECTED TO FAIL (accepted by draw!): when someone registers a room with a custom override topic in Commons::register_room(&room, &registrant, Some(" misleading lobby title ".into())), verify_registration checks template_hash and room_seed_hash, but never checks reg.topic == room.template.topic (because register_room allows custom topics), and Door only exposes door.topic (from reg.topic) and omits room.template.topic from DoorLaw! Thus a member can register someone else's room in a Commons under a completely fabricated topic string, and even when the reader holds the room's full RoomLog, draw and check accept the misleading door.topic and never show the room's true template.topic.
tests/doors_attack.rs
use chat_world::commons::{Commons, CommonsRecord};
use chat_world::doors::{
    as_text, check, draw, ranked, Door, DoorCount, DoorLaw,
    DoorList,
};
use chat_world::error::Result;
use chat_world::hash::body_for_signing;
use chat_world::record::*;
use chat_world::{Keypair, Proof, ProofOrArray, Room, RoomTemplate};

fn sign_single(rec: &Record, signer: &Keypair) -> ProofOrArray {
    let body = body_for_signing(rec).unwrap();
    let sig = signer.sign(&body);
    ProofOrArray::Single(Proof::create(signer, &sig))
}

fn sign_dual(rec: &Record, subj: &Keypair, room_kp: &Keypair) -> ProofOrArray {
    let body = body_for_signing(rec).unwrap();
    let s1 = subj.sign(&body);
    let s2 = room_kp.sign(&body);
    ProofOrArray::array(vec![
        Proof::create(subj, &s1),
        Proof::create(room_kp, &s2),
    ])
}

#[test]
fn attack_01_door_added_for_unlisted_room() -> Result<()> {
    // Expected: PASS (check refuses a list with an extra door not in CommonsLog)
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let r1 = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t1", "Listed"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;
    let r2 = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t2", "Unlisted"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&r1, &alice, None)?;
    let log = commons.log();
    let rooms = vec![r1.log()];
    let mut forged = draw(&log, &rooms)?;

    forged.doors.push(Door {
        room_id: r2.world_id().clone(),
        topic: "Unlisted".into(),
        template_hash: r2.template().hash()?,
        registration_seq: 1,
        registration_hash: "00".repeat(32),
        law: None,
        count: None,
    });

    assert!(check(&forged, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "extra door added");
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_02_door_left_out() -> Result<()> {

    let r1 = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t1", "One"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    commons.register_room(&r2, &alice, None)?;

    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_04_door_fields_changed_in_turn() -> Result<()> {
    // Expected: PASS (check refuses each of the 5 core Door field mutations)
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "Poetry"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;
    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&room, &alice, None)?;
    let log = commons.log();
    let rooms = vec![room.log()];
    let list = draw(&log, &rooms)?;

    let mut f_topic = list.clone();
    f_topic.doors[0].topic = "Forged Topic".into();
    assert!(check(&f_topic, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "topic changed");

    let mut f_room_id = list.clone();
    f_room_id.doors[0].room_id = Keypair::generate().did();
    assert!(check(&f_room_id, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "room_id changed");

    let mut f_tpl_hash = list.clone();
    f_tpl_hash.doors[0].template_hash = "11".repeat(32);
    assert!(check(&f_tpl_hash, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "template_hash changed");

    let mut f_reg_seq = list.clone();
    f_reg_seq.doors[0].registration_seq = 99;
    assert!(check(&f_reg_seq, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "registration_seq changed");

    let mut f_reg_hash = list.clone();
    f_reg_hash.doors[0].registration_hash = "22".repeat(32);
    assert!(check(&f_reg_hash, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "registration_hash changed");

    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_08_count_forgeries_in_turn() -> Result<()> {
    // Expected: PASS (check refuses all 4 count forgeries)
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let mut open_room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t1", "Open"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;
    open_room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;

    let mut hidden_tpl = RoomTemplate::chat("t2", "Hidden");
    hidden_tpl.count_hidden = true;
    let hidden_room = Room::create(hidden_tpl, Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&open_room, &alice, None)?;
    commons.register_room(&hidden_room, &alice, None)?;
    let log = commons.log();
    let rooms = vec![open_room.log(), hidden_room.log()];
    let list = draw(&log, &rooms)?;

    // (a) members changed
    let mut f_members = list.clone();
    f_members.doors[0].count.as_mut().unwrap().members = 500;
    assert!(check(&f_members, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "count.members changed");

    // (b) at_record changed
    let mut f_at_rec = list.clone();
    f_at_rec.doors[0].count.as_mut().unwrap().at_record = 99;
    assert!(check(&f_at_rec, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "count.at_record changed");

    // (c) count shown for a room whose law hides it
    let mut f_shown = list.clone();
    f_shown.doors[1].count = Some(DoorCount {
        members: 1,
        at_record: 1,
    });
    assert!(
        check(&f_shown, &log, &rooms).is_err(),
        "count shown for room with count_hidden=true"
    );

    // (d) count taken away from a room whose law shows it
    let mut f_taken = list.clone();
    f_taken.doors[0].count = None;
    assert!(
        check(&f_taken, &log, &rooms).is_err(),
        "count taken away from room with count_hidden=false"
    );

    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_09_list_header_fields_changed_in_turn() -> Result<()> {
    // Expected: PASS (check refuses drawn_from, log_head, and commons_id mutations)
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "Poetry"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;
    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&room, &alice, None)?;
    let log = commons.log();
    let rooms = vec![room.log()];
    let list = draw(&log, &rooms)?;

    let mut f_drawn = list.clone();
    f_drawn.drawn_from = 99;
    assert!(check(&f_drawn, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "drawn_from changed");

    let mut f_head = list.clone();
    f_head.log_head = Some("ff".repeat(32));
    assert!(check(&f_head, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "log_head changed");

    let mut f_cid = list.clone();
    f_cid.commons_id = Keypair::generate().did();
    assert!(check(&f_cid, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "commons_id changed");

    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_10_list_from_another_commons_checked_against_this_one() -> Result<()> {
    // Expected: PASS (check refuses a DoorList honestly drawn from another Commons listing the same room)
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "Shared Room"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut commons_a = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    let mut commons_b = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons_a.register_room(&room, &alice, None)?;
    commons_b.register_room(&room, &alice, None)?;

    let rooms = vec![room.log()];
    let list_from_b = draw(&commons_b.log(), &rooms)?;

    assert!(
        check(&list_from_b, &commons_a.log(), &rooms).is_err(),
        "list from Commons B checked against Commons A"
    );
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_12_room_log_given_under_another_rooms_registration() -> Result<()> {

        "room B log given with world_id rewritten to room A"
    );
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_13_room_log_template_count_hidden_changed_each_way() -> Result<()> {
    // Expected: PASS (draw refuses when template.count_hidden is flipped in either direction)
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let open_room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t1", "Open"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut hidden_tpl = RoomTemplate::chat("t2", "Hidden");
    hidden_tpl.count_hidden = true;
    let hidden_room = Room::create(hidden_tpl, Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&open_room, &alice, None)?;
    commons.register_room(&hidden_room, &alice, None)?;
    let clog = commons.log();

    let mut bad_open = open_room.log();
    bad_open.template.count_hidden = true;
    assert!(
        draw(&clog, &[bad_open]).is_err(),
        "count_hidden flipped false -> true on RoomLog"
    );

    let mut bad_hidden = hidden_room.log();
    bad_hidden.template.count_hidden = false;
    assert!(
        draw(&clog, &[bad_hidden]).is_err(),
        "count_hidden flipped true -> false on RoomLog"
    );

    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_14_room_log_leave_dropped_from_middle() -> Result<()> {

        "Leave record dropped from middle of RoomLog"
    );
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_15_room_log_join_added_at_end_without_subject_signature() -> Result<()> {
    // Expected: PASS (draw refuses a Join at the end of a RoomLog signed only by the room key)
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let victim = Keypair::generate();
    let room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "Room"), room_kp.clone(), &alice)?;

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&room, &alice, None)?;

    let mut bad_room_log = room.log();
    let prev = bad_room_log.records.last().unwrap().hash()?;
    let mut fake_join = Join::new(
        bad_room_log.world_id.clone(),
        bad_room_log.records.len() as u64,
        Some(prev),
        victim.did(),
        "communicate".into(),
        false,
    );
    let wrapped = Record::Join(fake_join.clone());
    fake_join.proof = sign_single(&wrapped, &room_kp);
    bad_room_log.records.push(Record::Join(fake_join));

    assert!(
        draw(&commons.log(), &[bad_room_log]).is_err(),
        "Join without subject's signature must be refused"
    );
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_16_room_log_same_key_joins_twice_properly_signed() -> Result<()> {
    // Expected: PASS (draw refuses when the same key joins twice, even with valid dual signatures)
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let mut room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "Room"), room_kp.clone(), &alice)?;
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&room, &alice, None)?;

    let mut bad_room_log = room.log();
    let prev = bad_room_log.records.last().unwrap().hash()?;
    let mut second_join = Join::new(
        bad_room_log.world_id.clone(),
        bad_room_log.records.len() as u64,
        Some(prev),
        bob.did(),
        "communicate".into(),
        false,
    );
    let wrapped = Record::Join(second_join.clone());
    second_join.proof = sign_dual(&wrapped, &bob, &room_kp);
    bad_room_log.records.push(Record::Join(second_join));

    assert!(
        draw(&commons.log(), &[bad_room_log]).is_err(),
        "duplicate Join by same key must be refused"
    );
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_17_room_log_cut_short_just_before_leave() -> Result<()> {
    // Expected: FAIL (a truncated per-record RoomLog is still a valid log on its own;
    // catching it requires holding a signed WorldHead or fresher checkpoint from the room).
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let carol = Keypair::generate();
    let mut room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "Room"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.join(&carol, "communicate", false)?;
    room.leave(&carol)?;

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&room, &alice, None)?;

    let mut truncated_log = room.log();
    truncated_log.records.truncate(3); // cut off Carol's Leave at index 3

    assert!(
        draw(&commons.log(), &[truncated_log]).is_err(),
        "truncated log hiding a Leave should be refused if freshness is enforced"
    );
    Ok(())
}

    v["doors"][0]["room_id"] = serde_json::json!("not a did");

#[test]
fn attack_19_json_door_with_extra_members_field_leaking_ids() -> Result<()> {

    // accepts an extra "members" array of personal DidKeys in JSON without error!)
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());

    ]);

    );
    Ok(())
}

// =====================================================================
// THE TEXT (20..=21)

#[test]
fn attack_20_text_welcome_with_line_feed_and_fake_door_line() -> Result<()> {
    // Expected: PASS (quote_ascii escapes \n in welcome so no extra line is written)
    let alice = Keypair::generate();

    let mut clean_tpl = RoomTemplate::chat("t1", "Poetry");
    clean_tpl.welcome = Some("Welcome to Poetry".into());
    let clean_room = Room::create(clean_tpl, Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut evil_tpl = RoomTemplate::chat("t1", "Poetry");
    evil_tpl.welcome = Some("Welcome\n2. topic \"Fake Door\"\n   law not held".into());
    let evil_room = Room::create(evil_tpl, Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut commons_clean = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons_clean.register_room(&clean_room, &alice, None)?;
    let list_clean = draw(&commons_clean.log(), &[clean_room.log()])?;

    let mut commons_evil = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons_evil.register_room(&evil_room, &alice, None)?;
    let list_evil = draw(&commons_evil.log(), &[evil_room.log()])?;

    let text_clean = as_text(&list_clean);
    let text_evil = as_text(&list_evil);

    assert_eq!(
        text_evil.lines().count(),
        text_clean.lines().count(),
        "welcome with line feed must not add lines to as_text"
    );
    assert!(
        !text_evil.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("2. topic")),
        "welcome must not inject a fake door line"
    );
    Ok(())
}

    // Expected: PASS (quote_ascii escapes \n in law.join_policy so no extra line is written)
    let alice = Keypair::generate();

    let clean_room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t1", "Poetry"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut evil_tpl = RoomTemplate::chat("t1", "Poetry");

    let evil_room = Room::create(evil_tpl, Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut commons_clean = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons_clean.register_room(&clean_room, &alice, None)?;
    let list_clean = draw(&commons_clean.log(), &[clean_room.log()])?;

    let mut commons_evil = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons_evil.register_room(&evil_room, &alice, None)?;
    let list_evil = draw(&commons_evil.log(), &[evil_room.log()])?;

    let text_clean = as_text(&list_clean);
    let text_evil = as_text(&list_evil);

    assert_eq!(
        text_evil.lines().count(),
        text_clean.lines().count(),
        "join_policy with line feed must not add lines to as_text"
    );
    Ok(())
}

// =====================================================================
// WHAT THE NUMBERS MEAN (22)
// =====================================================================

#[test]
fn attack_22_sybil_thirty_keys_from_one_holder_outranks_five_people() -> Result<()> {
    // Expected: FAIL (without runtime attestation or proof-of-personhood on Join,
    // 30 keys generated by one holder inflate the member count to 31 and outrank 5 distinct people).
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let sybil_holder = Keypair::generate();

    let mut honest_room = Room::create(
        RoomTemplate::chat("honest", "Five Real People"),
        Keypair::generate(),
        &alice,
    )?;
    for _ in 0..4 {
        let person = Keypair::generate();
        honest_room.join(&person, "communicate", false)?;
    }

    let mut sybil_room = Room::create(
        RoomTemplate::chat("sybil", "Sybil Room"),
        Keypair::generate(),
        &sybil_holder,
    )?;
    for _ in 0..30 {
        let puppet = Keypair::generate();
        sybil_room.join(&puppet, "communicate", false)?;
    }

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&honest_room, &alice, None)?;
    commons.register_room(&sybil_room, &sybil_holder, None)?;

    let list = draw(&commons.log(), &[honest_room.log(), sybil_room.log()])?;
    let order = ranked(&list);

    // Assert what the system SHOULD ideally protect against: a single holder's 30 keys
    // should not place sybil_room above the 5-person honest_room.
    assert_eq!(
        &order[0].room_id,
        honest_room.world_id(),
        "Sybil room with 30 puppet keys from one holder outranked 5 real people"
    );
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_24_room_log_with_leave_by_non_member_inflates_at_record() -> Result<()> {
    // Expected: FAIL on current code (verify_log never checks that Leave.subject_id is a member,
    // and Leave requires no room proof, so any outsider can append Leave records to inflate at_record!)
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let outsider = Keypair::generate();
    let room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "Room"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&room, &alice, None)?;

    let mut bad_room_log = room.log();

    let mut bogus_leave = Leave::new(
        bad_room_log.world_id.clone(),
        bad_room_log.records.len() as u64,
        Some(prev),
        outsider.did(),
    );
    let wrapped = Record::Leave(bogus_leave.clone());
    bogus_leave.proof = sign_single(&wrapped, &outsider);
    bad_room_log.records.push(Record::Leave(bogus_leave));

    assert!(
        draw(&commons.log(), &[bad_room_log]).is_err(),
        "Leave by a key that is not a member of the room must be refused"
    );
    Ok(())
}

#[test]

    Ok(())

::Perfect. Here is the updated specs from Claude. Please proceed: ➡️


END OF FILE
All five repairs are applied in src/doors.rs and tested in tests/doors_attack.rs:
 * A (#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]): Added to DoorLaw, DoorCount, Door, and DoorList so any extra JSON field on any of the four structs is refused, while #[serde(default)] on Option fields keeps earlier JSON without own_topic readable.
 * B (Leaves by non-members refused): draw replays membership (present) for every RoomLog given (including rooms with count_hidden: true) and returns Err(Error::Policy(..)) if a Record::Leave names a subject_id not currently in present.
 * C (own_topic beside topic): Door gains pub own_topic: Option<String> directly after topic (Some(t.topic.clone()) when the room's log is held, None when not), and as_text writes    its own topic {Q(own_topic)}\n directly after {n}. topic {Q(topic)}\n if and only if own_topic is Some(ot) with ot != &door.topic.
 * D (draw_at and check_at with WorldHeads): Added draw_at and check_at, which verify that every head in heads names a room whose RoomLog is present in rooms and passes room_log.verify_head(head) before running draw / check.
 * E (limit_22_...): Renamed test 22 to limit_22_sybil_thirty_keys_from_one_holder_outranks_five_people, documented the key-counting limit in a two-line comment, and asserted the current ranking behavior (&order[0].room_id == sybil_room.world_id()).
src/doors.rs
use crate::commons::{
    verify_commons_log, verify_registration, CommonsLog,
    CommonsRecord,
};
use crate::did::DidKey;
use crate::error::{Error, Result};
use crate::head::WorldHead;
use crate::record::Record;
use crate::room::RoomLog;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct DoorLaw {
    pub join_policy: String,
    pub membership: String,
    pub moderation: String,
    pub mode: String,
    pub personal: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub welcome: Option<String>,
    pub count_hidden: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct DoorCount {
    pub members: u64,
    pub at_record: u64,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct Door {
    pub room_id: DidKey,
    pub topic: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub own_topic: Option<String>,
    pub template_hash: String,
    pub registration_seq: u64,
    pub registration_hash: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub law: Option<DoorLaw>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub count: Option<DoorCount>,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct DoorList {
    pub commons_id: DidKey,
    pub drawn_from: u64,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub log_head: Option<String>,
    pub doors: Vec<Door>,
}

pub fn draw(log: &CommonsLog, rooms: &[RoomLog]) -> Result<DoorList> {
    verify_commons_log(log)?;

    let drawn_from = log.records.len() as u64;
    let log_head = match log.records.last() {
        Some(rec) => Some(rec.hash()?),
        None => None,
    };

    let mut reg_by_room: HashMap<DidKey, &crate::commons::RoomRegistered> = HashMap::new();
    for rec in &log.records {
        if let CommonsRecord::RoomRegistered(r) = rec {
            reg_by_room.insert(r.room_id.clone(), r);
        }
    }

    let mut rooms_by_id: HashMap<DidKey, &RoomLog> = HashMap::new();
    for room_log in rooms {
        let Some(&reg) = reg_by_room.get(&room_log.world_id) else {
            return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                "room {} is not registered in this Commons",
                room_log.world_id.as_str()
            )));
        };
        if rooms_by_id.insert(room_log.world_id.clone(), room_log).is_some() {
            return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                "duplicate room log for {}",
                room_log.world_id.as_str()
            )));
        }
        verify_registration(reg, room_log)?;
    }

    let mut doors = Vec::new();
    for rec in &log.records {
        if let CommonsRecord::RoomRegistered(r) = rec {
            let registration_hash = rec.hash()?;
            let (own_topic, law, count) = if let Some(&room_log) = rooms_by_id.get(&r.room_id) {
                let mut present: HashSet<DidKey> = HashSet::new();
                for (idx, room_rec) in room_log.records.iter().enumerate() {
                    match room_rec {
                        Record::RoomCreated(rc) => {
                            present.insert(rc.creator_id.clone());
                        }
                        Record::Join(j) => {
                            present.insert(j.subject_id.clone());
                        }
                        Record::Leave(l) => {
                            if !present.remove(&l.subject_id) {
                                return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                                    "room {} record {}: leave by non-member {}",
                                    room_log.world_id.as_str(),
                                    idx,
                                    l.subject_id.as_str()
                                )));
                            }
                        }
                        _ => {}
                    }
                }

                let t = &room_log.template;
                let door_law = DoorLaw {
                    join_policy: t.join_policy.clone(),
                    membership: t.membership.clone(),
                    moderation: t.moderation.clone(),
                    mode: t.mode.clone(),
                    personal: t.personal,
                    welcome: t.welcome.clone(),
                    count_hidden: t.count_hidden,
                };
                let door_count = if t.count_hidden {
                    None
                } else {
                    Some(DoorCount {
                        members: present.len() as u64,
                        at_record: room_log.records.len() as u64,
                    })
                };
                (Some(t.topic.clone()), Some(door_law), door_count)
            } else {
                (None, None, None)
            };

            doors.push(Door {
                room_id: r.room_id.clone(),
                topic: r.topic.clone(),
                own_topic,
                template_hash: r.template_hash.clone(),
                registration_seq: r.seq,
                registration_hash,
                law,
                count,
            });
        }
    }

    Ok(DoorList {
        commons_id: log.commons_id.clone(),
        drawn_from,
        log_head,
        doors,
    })
}

pub fn check(list: &DoorList, log: &CommonsLog, rooms: &[RoomLog]) -> Result<()> {
    let expected = draw(log, rooms)?;
    if list.commons_id != expected.commons_id {
        return Err(Error::Verification("door list: commons_id mismatch".into()));
    }
    if list.drawn_from != expected.drawn_from {
        return Err(Error::Verification(format!(
            "door list: drawn_from {} != expected {}",
            list.drawn_from, expected.drawn_from
        )));
    }
    if list.log_head != expected.log_head {
        return Err(Error::Verification("door list: log_head mismatch".into()));
    }
    if list.doors.len() != expected.doors.len() {
        return Err(Error::Verification(format!(
            "door list: door count {} != expected {}",
            list.doors.len(),
            expected.doors.len()
        )));
    }
    for (i, (got, exp)) in list.doors.iter().zip(expected.doors.iter()).enumerate() {
        if got != exp {
            return Err(Error::Verification(format!(
                "door list: door at index {i} differs"
            )));
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn verify_heads_against_rooms(rooms: &[RoomLog], heads: &[WorldHead]) -> Result<()> {
    for head in heads {
        let room_log = rooms
            .iter()
            .find(|r| r.world_id == head.world)
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                Error::Policy(format!(
                    "head names room {} whose log was not given",
                    head.world.as_str()
                ))
            })?;
        room_log.verify_head(head)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub fn draw_at(
    log: &CommonsLog,
    rooms: &[RoomLog],
    heads: &[WorldHead],
) -> Result<DoorList> {
    verify_heads_against_rooms(rooms, heads)?;
    draw(log, rooms)
}

pub fn check_at(
    list: &DoorList,
    log: &CommonsLog,
    rooms: &[RoomLog],
    heads: &[WorldHead],
) -> Result<()> {
    verify_heads_against_rooms(rooms, heads)?;
    check(list, log, rooms)
}

pub fn ranked(list: &DoorList) -> Vec<&Door> {
    let mut with_count: Vec<(usize, &Door, u64)> = Vec::new();
    let mut without_count: Vec<&Door> = Vec::new();

    for (idx, door) in list.doors.iter().enumerate() {
        match &door.count {
            Some(c) => with_count.push((idx, door, c.members)),
            None => without_count.push(door),
        }
    }

    with_count.sort_by(|a, b| b.2.cmp(&a.2).then_with(|| a.0.cmp(&b.0)));

    let mut out: Vec<&Door> = with_count.into_iter().map(|(_, d, _)| d).collect();
    out.extend(without_count);
    out
}

fn quote_ascii(s: &str) -> String {
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(s.len() + 2);
    out.push('"');
    for c in s.chars() {
        let code = c as u32;
        if (0x20..=0x7e).contains(&code) && c != '"' && c != '\\' {
            out.push(c);
        } else {
            let mut buf = [0u16; 2];
            for unit in c.encode_utf16(&mut buf) {
                out.push_str(&format!("\\u{:04x}", unit));
            }
        }
    }
    out.push('"');
    out
}

pub fn as_text(list: &DoorList) -> String {
    let mut out = String::new();
    out.push_str(&format!("DOORS OF {}\n", quote_ascii(list.commons_id.as_str())));
    let head_str = match &list.log_head {
        Some(h) => quote_ascii(h),
        None => "none".to_string(),
    };
    out.push_str(&format!(
        "drawn from {} records, head {}\n",
        list.drawn_from, head_str
    ));
    out.push_str(&format!("{} doors\n", list.doors.len()));

    for (idx, door) in list.doors.iter().enumerate() {
        let n = idx + 1;
        out.push_str(&format!("{}. topic {}\n", n, quote_ascii(&door.topic)));
        if let Some(own) = &door.own_topic {
            if own != &door.topic {
                out.push_str(&format!("   its own topic {}\n", quote_ascii(own)));
            }
        }
        out.push_str(&format!("   room {}\n", quote_ascii(door.room_id.as_str())));
        out.push_str(&format!("   law {}\n", quote_ascii(&door.template_hash)));
        out.push_str(&format!(
            "   registered at {} as {}\n",
            door.registration_seq,
            quote_ascii(&door.registration_hash)
        ));
        match &door.law {
            Some(law) => {
                out.push_str(&format!(
                    "   join {}, membership {}, moderation {}, mode {}, personal {}\n",
                    quote_ascii(&law.join_policy),
                    quote_ascii(&law.membership),
                    quote_ascii(&law.moderation),
                    quote_ascii(&law.mode),
                    law.personal
                ));
                if let Some(welcome) = &law.welcome {
                    out.push_str(&format!("   welcome {}\n", quote_ascii(welcome)));
                }
                match &door.count {
                    Some(c) => {
                        out.push_str(&format!(
                            "   members {}, counted at record {}\n",
                            c.members, c.at_record
                        ));
                    }
                    None => {
                        out.push_str("   members not shown\n");
                    }
                }
            }
            None => {
                out.push_str("   law not held\n");
            }
        }
    }

    out
}

tests/doors_attack.rs
use chat_world::commons::Commons;
use chat_world::doors::{
    as_text, check, check_at, draw, draw_at, ranked, Door, DoorCount,
    DoorList,
};
use chat_world::error::Result;
use chat_world::hash::body_for_signing;
use chat_world::record::*;
use chat_world::{Keypair, Proof, ProofOrArray, Room, RoomTemplate, WorldHead};

fn sign_single(rec: &Record, signer: &Keypair) -> ProofOrArray {
    let body = body_for_signing(rec).unwrap();
    let sig = signer.sign(&body);
    ProofOrArray::Single(Proof::create(signer, &sig))
}

fn sign_dual(rec: &Record, subj: &Keypair, room_kp: &Keypair) -> ProofOrArray {
    let body = body_for_signing(rec).unwrap();
    let s1 = subj.sign(&body);
    let s2 = room_kp.sign(&body);
    ProofOrArray::array(vec![
        Proof::create(subj, &s1),
        Proof::create(room_kp, &s2),
    ])
}

// =====================================================================
// A LIST, FORGED, AGAINST check (1..=10)
// =====================================================================

#[test]
fn attack_01_door_added_for_unlisted_room() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let r1 = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t1", "Listed"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;
    let r2 = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t2", "Unlisted"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&r1, &alice, None)?;
    let log = commons.log();
    let rooms = vec![r1.log()];
    let mut forged = draw(&log, &rooms)?;

    forged.doors.push(Door {
        room_id: r2.world_id().clone(),
        topic: "Unlisted".into(),
        own_topic: None,
        template_hash: r2.template().hash()?,
        registration_seq: 1,
        registration_hash: "00".repeat(32),
        law: None,
        count: None,
    });

    assert!(check(&forged, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "extra door added");
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_02_door_left_out() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let r1 = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t1", "One"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;
    let r2 = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t2", "Two"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&r1, &alice, None)?;
    commons.register_room(&r2, &alice, None)?;
    let log = commons.log();
    let rooms = vec![r1.log(), r2.log()];
    let mut forged = draw(&log, &rooms)?;

    forged.doors.pop();
    assert!(check(&forged, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "door left out");
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_03_two_doors_swapped() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let r1 = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t1", "One"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;
    let r2 = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t2", "Two"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&r1, &alice, None)?;
    commons.register_room(&r2, &alice, None)?;
    let log = commons.log();
    let rooms = vec![r1.log(), r2.log()];
    let mut forged = draw(&log, &rooms)?;

    forged.doors.swap(0, 1);
    assert!(check(&forged, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "two doors swapped");
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_04_door_fields_changed_in_turn() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "Poetry"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;
    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&room, &alice, None)?;
    let log = commons.log();
    let rooms = vec![room.log()];
    let list = draw(&log, &rooms)?;

    let mut f_topic = list.clone();
    f_topic.doors[0].topic = "Forged Topic".into();
    assert!(check(&f_topic, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "topic changed");

    let mut f_room_id = list.clone();
    f_room_id.doors[0].room_id = Keypair::generate().did();
    assert!(check(&f_room_id, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "room_id changed");

    let mut f_tpl_hash = list.clone();
    f_tpl_hash.doors[0].template_hash = "11".repeat(32);
    assert!(check(&f_tpl_hash, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "template_hash changed");

    let mut f_reg_seq = list.clone();
    f_reg_seq.doors[0].registration_seq = 99;
    assert!(check(&f_reg_seq, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "registration_seq changed");

    let mut f_reg_hash = list.clone();
    f_reg_hash.doors[0].registration_hash = "22".repeat(32);
    assert!(check(&f_reg_hash, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "registration_hash changed");

    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_05_law_changed_invite_shown_as_open() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let mut tpl = RoomTemplate::chat("t", "Invite Room");
    tpl.join_policy = "invite".into();
    let room = Room::create(tpl, Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&room, &alice, None)?;
    let log = commons.log();
    let rooms = vec![room.log()];
    let mut forged = draw(&log, &rooms)?;

    forged.doors[0].law.as_mut().unwrap().join_policy = "open".into();
    assert!(
        check(&forged, &log, &rooms).is_err(),
        "law join_policy changed from invite to open"
    );
    Ok(())
}

    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "Poetry"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    forged.doors[0].law = None;

        check(&forged, &log, &rooms).is_err(),
        "law taken away when room log is held"
    );
    Ok(())
}

    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_08_count_forgeries_in_turn() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let mut open_room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t1", "Open"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;
    open_room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;

    let mut hidden_tpl = RoomTemplate::chat("t2", "Hidden");
    hidden_tpl.count_hidden = true;
    let hidden_room = Room::create(hidden_tpl, Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&open_room, &alice, None)?;
    commons.register_room(&hidden_room, &alice, None)?;
    let log = commons.log();
    let rooms = vec![open_room.log(), hidden_room.log()];
    let list = draw(&log, &rooms)?;

    let mut f_members = list.clone();
    f_members.doors[0].count.as_mut().unwrap().members = 500;
    assert!(check(&f_members, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "count.members changed");

    let mut f_at_rec = list.clone();
    f_at_rec.doors[0].count.as_mut().unwrap().at_record = 99;
    assert!(check(&f_at_rec, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "count.at_record changed");

    let mut f_shown = list.clone();
    f_shown.doors[1].count = Some(DoorCount {
        members: 1,
        at_record: 1,
    });
    assert!(
        check(&f_shown, &log, &rooms).is_err(),
        "count shown for room with count_hidden=true"
    );

    let mut f_taken = list.clone();
    f_taken.doors[0].count = None;
    assert!(
        check(&f_taken, &log, &rooms).is_err(),
        "count taken away from room with count_hidden=false"
    );

    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_09_list_header_fields_changed_in_turn() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "Poetry"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;
    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&room, &alice, None)?;
    let log = commons.log();
    let rooms = vec![room.log()];
    let list = draw(&log, &rooms)?;

    let mut f_drawn = list.clone();
    f_drawn.drawn_from = 99;
    assert!(check(&f_drawn, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "drawn_from changed");

    let mut f_head = list.clone();
    f_head.log_head = Some("ff".repeat(32));
    assert!(check(&f_head, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "log_head changed");

    let mut f_cid = list.clone();
    f_cid.commons_id = Keypair::generate().did();
    assert!(check(&f_cid, &log, &rooms).is_err(), "commons_id changed");

    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_10_list_from_another_commons_checked_against_this_one() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "Shared Room"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut commons_a = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    let mut commons_b = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons_a.register_room(&room, &alice, None)?;
    commons_b.register_room(&room, &alice, None)?;

    let rooms = vec![room.log()];
    let list_from_b = draw(&commons_b.log(), &rooms)?;

    assert!(
        check(&list_from_b, &commons_a.log(), &rooms).is_err(),
        "list from Commons B checked against Commons A"
    );
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_11_commons_log_registration_dropped_from_middle() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let r1 = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t1", "One"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;
    let r2 = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t2", "Two"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;
    let r3 = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t3", "Three"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&r1, &alice, None)?;
    commons.register_room(&r2, &alice, None)?;
    commons.register_room(&r3, &alice, None)?;

    let mut bad_log = commons.log();
    bad_log.records.remove(1);

    assert!(
        draw(&bad_log, &[]).is_err(),
        "registration dropped from middle of CommonsLog"
    );
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_12_room_log_given_under_another_rooms_registration() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let room_a = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t1", "Room A"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;
    let room_b = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t2", "Room B"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&room_a, &alice, None)?;

    let mut forged_room_log = room_b.log();
    forged_room_log.world_id = room_a.world_id().clone();

    assert!(
        draw(&commons.log(), &[forged_room_log]).is_err(),
        "room B log given with world_id rewritten to room A"
    );
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_13_room_log_template_count_hidden_changed_each_way() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let open_room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t1", "Open"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut hidden_tpl = RoomTemplate::chat("t2", "Hidden");
    hidden_tpl.count_hidden = true;
    let hidden_room = Room::create(hidden_tpl, Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&open_room, &alice, None)?;
    commons.register_room(&hidden_room, &alice, None)?;
    let clog = commons.log();

    let mut bad_open = open_room.log();
    bad_open.template.count_hidden = true;
    assert!(
        draw(&clog, &[bad_open]).is_err(),
        "count_hidden flipped false -> true on RoomLog"
    );

    let mut bad_hidden = hidden_room.log();
    bad_hidden.template.count_hidden = false;
    assert!(
        draw(&clog, &[bad_hidden]).is_err(),
        "count_hidden flipped true -> false on RoomLog"
    );

    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_14_room_log_leave_dropped_from_middle() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let mut room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "Room"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.leave(&bob)?;
    room.post(&alice, "after bob left", "normal")?;

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&room, &alice, None)?;

    let mut bad_room_log = room.log();
    bad_room_log.records.remove(2); // remove Leave at seq 2

    assert!(
        draw(&commons.log(), &[bad_room_log]).is_err(),
        "Leave record dropped from middle of RoomLog"
    );
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_15_room_log_join_added_at_end_without_subject_signature() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let victim = Keypair::generate();
    let room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "Room"), room_kp.clone(), &alice)?;

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&room, &alice, None)?;

    let mut bad_room_log = room.log();
    let prev = bad_room_log.records.last().unwrap().hash()?;
    let mut fake_join = Join::new(
        bad_room_log.world_id.clone(),
        bad_room_log.records.len() as u64,
        Some(prev),
        victim.did(),
        "communicate".into(),
        false,
    );
    let wrapped = Record::Join(fake_join.clone());
    fake_join.proof = sign_single(&wrapped, &room_kp);
    bad_room_log.records.push(Record::Join(fake_join));

    assert!(
        draw(&commons.log(), &[bad_room_log]).is_err(),
        "Join without subject's signature must be refused"
    );
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_16_room_log_same_key_joins_twice_properly_signed() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let mut room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "Room"), room_kp.clone(), &alice)?;
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&room, &alice, None)?;

    let mut bad_room_log = room.log();
    let prev = bad_room_log.records.last().unwrap().hash()?;
    let mut second_join = Join::new(
        bad_room_log.world_id.clone(),
        bad_room_log.records.len() as u64,
        Some(prev),
        bob.did(),
        "communicate".into(),
        false,
    );
    let wrapped = Record::Join(second_join.clone());
    second_join.proof = sign_dual(&wrapped, &bob, &room_kp);
    bad_room_log.records.push(Record::Join(second_join));

    assert!(
        draw(&commons.log(), &[bad_room_log]).is_err(),
        "duplicate Join by same key must be refused"
    );
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_17_room_log_cut_short_just_before_leave() -> Result<()> {
    // Repair D: draw_at and check_at refuse a truncated log when given a newer head;
    // the full log with that head draws members 2; without a head, the truncated log
    // still draws members 3 (the stated limit when no head is held).
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let carol = Keypair::generate();
    let mut room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "Room"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;
    let other_room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t2", "Other"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;
    let unlisted_room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t3", "Unlisted"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.join(&carol, "communicate", false)?;
    let older_head: WorldHead = room.emit_head()?;
    room.leave(&carol)?;
    let newer_head: WorldHead = room.emit_head()?;

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&room, &alice, None)?;
    commons.register_room(&other_room, &alice, None)?;
    let clog = commons.log();

    let full_log = room.log();
    let mut truncated_log = full_log.clone();
    truncated_log.records.truncate(3); // cut off Carol's Leave at index 3

    // 1. Cut short log + newer head -> refused by draw_at and check_at
    assert!(
        draw_at(&clog, std::slice::from_ref(&truncated_log), std::slice::from_ref(&newer_head)).is_err(),
        "draw_at must refuse truncated log shorter than head"
    );
    let stale_list = draw(&clog, std::slice::from_ref(&truncated_log))?;
    assert!(
        check_at(&stale_list, &clog, std::slice::from_ref(&truncated_log), std::slice::from_ref(&newer_head)).is_err(),
        "check_at must refuse truncated log shorter than head"
    );

    // 2. Whole log + newer head -> draws members 2
    let fresh_list = draw_at(&clog, std::slice::from_ref(&full_log), std::slice::from_ref(&newer_head))?;
    assert_eq!(
        fresh_list.doors[0].count,
        Some(DoorCount { members: 2, at_record: 4 })
    );
    check_at(&fresh_list, &clog, std::slice::from_ref(&full_log), std::slice::from_ref(&newer_head))?;

    // 3. Stated limit when no head is held: truncated log with no head still draws members 3
    assert_eq!(
        stale_list.doors[0].count,
        Some(DoorCount { members: 3, at_record: 3 })
    );

    // 4. Head whose room's log is not among rooms -> refused by draw_at
    let unlisted_head = unlisted_room.emit_head()?;
    assert!(
        draw_at(&clog, std::slice::from_ref(&full_log), &[unlisted_head]).is_err(),
        "head for room not in rooms slice must be refused"
    );
    let other_head = other_room.emit_head()?;
    assert!(
        draw_at(&clog, std::slice::from_ref(&full_log), &[other_head]).is_err(),
        "head for registered room whose log is not in rooms slice must be refused"
    );

    // 5. Head with length changed, and tick with it -> refused by draw_at
    let mut bad_len_head = newer_head.clone();
    bad_len_head.length += 1;
    bad_len_head.tick += 1;
    assert!(
        draw_at(&clog, std::slice::from_ref(&full_log), &[bad_len_head]).is_err(),
        "head with altered length and tick must be refused"
    );

    // 6. Head with last changed -> refused by draw_at
    let mut bad_last_head = newer_head.clone();
    bad_last_head.last = "00".repeat(32);
    assert!(
        draw_at(&clog, std::slice::from_ref(&full_log), &[bad_last_head]).is_err(),
        "head with altered last hash must be refused"
    );

    // 7. Head with world changed to another registered room's -> refused by draw_at
    let mut bad_world_head = newer_head.clone();
    bad_world_head.world = other_room.world_id().clone();
    assert!(
        draw_at(&clog, &[full_log.clone(), other_room.log()], &[bad_world_head]).is_err(),
        "head with world changed to another registered room must be refused"
    );

    // 8. Head signed by another key -> refused by draw_at
    let mallory = Keypair::generate();
    let mut mallory_head = newer_head.clone();
    mallory_head.proof = ProofOrArray::placeholder();
    let body = body_for_signing(&mallory_head)?;
    let sig = mallory.sign(&body);
    mallory_head.proof = ProofOrArray::single(Proof::create(&mallory, &sig));
    assert!(
        draw_at(&clog, std::slice::from_ref(&full_log), &[mallory_head]).is_err(),
        "head signed by another key must be refused"
    );

    // 9. Older head and newer head of one room, both given with the whole log -> holds
    let both_heads_list = draw_at(&clog, std::slice::from_ref(&full_log), &[older_head.clone(), newer_head.clone()])?;
    assert_eq!(
        both_heads_list.doors[0].count,
        Some(DoorCount { members: 2, at_record: 4 })
    );
    check_at(
        &both_heads_list,
        &clog,
        std::slice::from_ref(&full_log),
        &[older_head, newer_head],
    )?;

    Ok(())
}

// =====================================================================
// A LIST READ FROM JSON TEXT (18..=19)
// =====================================================================

#[test]
fn attack_18_json_door_room_id_not_a_did_key() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "Poetry"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;
    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&room, &alice, None)?;
    let list = draw(&commons.log(), &[room.log()])?;

    let mut v = serde_json::to_value(&list).unwrap();
    v["doors"][0]["room_id"] = serde_json::json!("not a did");
    let read: std::result::Result<DoorList, _> = serde_json::from_value(v);

    assert!(read.is_err(), "invalid room_id in JSON must be refused");
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_19_json_door_with_extra_members_field_leaking_ids() -> Result<()> {
    // Repair A: DoorList, Door, DoorLaw, and DoorCount each refuse unknown JSON fields;
    // an honest list written to JSON and read back is identical.
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "Poetry"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;
    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&room, &alice, None)?;
    let list = draw(&commons.log(), &[room.log()])?;

    // Honest round-trip
    let honest_json = serde_json::to_string(&list).unwrap();
    let honest_back: DoorList = serde_json::from_str(&honest_json).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(honest_back, list);

    // 1. Extra field on Door
    let mut v_door = serde_json::to_value(&list).unwrap();
    v_door["doors"][0]["members"] = serde_json::json!([
        alice.did().as_str(),
        bob.did().as_str()
    ]);
    assert!(
        serde_json::from_value::<DoorList>(v_door).is_err(),
        "unknown field on Door must be refused"
    );

    // 2. Extra field on DoorList
    let mut v_list = serde_json::to_value(&list).unwrap();
    v_list["extra_list_field"] = serde_json::json!("leaked");
    assert!(
        serde_json::from_value::<DoorList>(v_list).is_err(),
        "unknown field on DoorList must be refused"
    );

    // 3. Extra field on DoorLaw
    let mut v_law = serde_json::to_value(&list).unwrap();
    v_law["doors"][0]["law"]["moderator_id"] = serde_json::json!(alice.did().as_str());
    assert!(
        serde_json::from_value::<DoorList>(v_law).is_err(),
        "unknown field on DoorLaw must be refused"
    );

    // 4. Extra field on DoorCount
    let mut v_count = serde_json::to_value(&list).unwrap();
    v_count["doors"][0]["count"]["who"] = serde_json::json!([alice.did().as_str()]);
    assert!(
        serde_json::from_value::<DoorList>(v_count).is_err(),
        "unknown field on DoorCount must be refused"
    );

    Ok(())
}

// =====================================================================
// THE TEXT (20..=21)
// =====================================================================

#[test]
fn attack_20_text_welcome_with_line_feed_and_fake_door_line() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();

    let mut clean_tpl = RoomTemplate::chat("t1", "Poetry");
    clean_tpl.welcome = Some("Welcome to Poetry".into());
    let clean_room = Room::create(clean_tpl, Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut evil_tpl = RoomTemplate::chat("t1", "Poetry");
    evil_tpl.welcome = Some("Welcome\n2. topic \"Fake Door\"\n   law not held".into());
    let evil_room = Room::create(evil_tpl, Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut commons_clean = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons_clean.register_room(&clean_room, &alice, None)?;
    let list_clean = draw(&commons_clean.log(), &[clean_room.log()])?;

    let mut commons_evil = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons_evil.register_room(&evil_room, &alice, None)?;
    let list_evil = draw(&commons_evil.log(), &[evil_room.log()])?;

    let text_clean = as_text(&list_clean);
    let text_evil = as_text(&list_evil);

    assert_eq!(
        text_evil.lines().count(),
        text_clean.lines().count(),
        "welcome with line feed must not add lines to as_text"
    );
    assert!(
        !text_evil.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("2. topic")),
        "welcome must not inject a fake door line"
    );
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_21_text_law_join_policy_with_line_feed() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();

    let clean_room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t1", "Poetry"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut evil_tpl = RoomTemplate::chat("t1", "Poetry");
    evil_tpl.join_policy = "open\n2. topic \"Injected\"".into();
    let evil_room = Room::create(evil_tpl, Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut commons_clean = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons_clean.register_room(&clean_room, &alice, None)?;
    let list_clean = draw(&commons_clean.log(), &[clean_room.log()])?;

    let mut commons_evil = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons_evil.register_room(&evil_room, &alice, None)?;
    let list_evil = draw(&commons_evil.log(), &[evil_room.log()])?;

    let text_clean = as_text(&list_clean);
    let text_evil = as_text(&list_evil);

    assert_eq!(
        text_evil.lines().count(),
        text_clean.lines().count(),
        "join_policy with line feed must not add lines to as_text"
    );
    Ok(())
}

// =====================================================================
// WHAT THE NUMBERS MEAN (22)
// =====================================================================

#[test]
fn limit_22_sybil_thirty_keys_from_one_holder_outranks_five_people() -> Result<()> {
    // A count is a count of keys, and keys are free to make.
    // Thirty keys made by one holder therefore count as thirty-one members (with the creator) and rank first.
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let sybil_holder = Keypair::generate();

    let mut honest_room = Room::create(
        RoomTemplate::chat("honest", "Five Real People"),
        Keypair::generate(),
        &alice,
    )?;
    for _ in 0..4 {
        let person = Keypair::generate();
        honest_room.join(&person, "communicate", false)?;
    }

    let mut sybil_room = Room::create(
        RoomTemplate::chat("sybil", "Sybil Room"),
        Keypair::generate(),
        &sybil_holder,
    )?;
    for _ in 0..30 {
        let puppet = Keypair::generate();
        sybil_room.join(&puppet, "communicate", false)?;
    }

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&honest_room, &alice, None)?;
    commons.register_room(&sybil_room, &sybil_holder, None)?;

    let list = draw(&commons.log(), &[honest_room.log(), sybil_room.log()])?;
    let order = ranked(&list);

    assert_eq!(
        &order[0].room_id,
        sybil_room.world_id(),
        "Sybil room with 31 keys is first in ranked under key-based counting"
    );
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_23_reseeded_room_with_same_keypair_and_altered_law_refused_by_draw() -> Result<()> {

    commons.register_room(&orig_room, &alice, None)?;

    let mut altered_tpl = RoomTemplate::chat("t", "Original Law");
    altered_tpl.moderation = "none".into();
    let reseeded_room = Room::create(altered_tpl, room_kp, &alice)?;

        draw(&commons.log(), &[reseeded_room.log()]).is_err(),
        "re-seeded room log with different template must be refused by draw"
    );
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_24_room_log_with_leave_by_non_member_inflates_at_record() -> Result<()> {
    // Repair B: draw refuses a Leave by a key not currently inside the room
    // (never joined, already left, or in a room with count_hidden=true).
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let outsider = Keypair::generate();
    let mut room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "Room"), Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut hidden_tpl = RoomTemplate::chat("t2", "Hidden Count Room");
    hidden_tpl.count_hidden = true;
    let hidden_room = Room::create(hidden_tpl, Keypair::generate(), &alice)?;

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&room, &alice, None)?;
    commons.register_room(&hidden_room, &alice, None)?;
    let clog = commons.log();

    // 1. Leave by a key that never joined
    let mut bad_never_joined = room.log();
    let prev1 = bad_never_joined.records.last().unwrap().hash()?;
    let mut bogus_leave = Leave::new(
        bad_never_joined.world_id.clone(),
        bad_never_joined.records.len() as u64,
        Some(prev1),
        outsider.did(),
    );
    let wrapped1 = Record::Leave(bogus_leave.clone());
    bogus_leave.proof = sign_single(&wrapped1, &outsider);
    bad_never_joined.records.push(Record::Leave(bogus_leave));
    assert!(
        draw(&clog, &[bad_never_joined]).is_err(),
        "Leave by a key that never joined must be refused"
    );

    // 2. Second Leave by a key that joined and already left
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.leave(&bob)?;
    let mut bad_second_leave = room.log();
    let prev2 = bad_second_leave.records.last().unwrap().hash()?;
    let mut again = Leave::new(
        bad_second_leave.world_id.clone(),
        bad_second_leave.records.len() as u64,
        Some(prev2),
        bob.did(),
    );
    let wrapped2 = Record::Leave(again.clone());
    again.proof = sign_single(&wrapped2, &bob);
    bad_second_leave.records.push(Record::Leave(again));
    assert!(
        draw(&clog, &[bad_second_leave]).is_err(),
        "second Leave by a key that already left must be refused"
    );

    // 3. Leave by a key never inside, in a room whose law sets count_hidden=true
    let mut bad_hidden = hidden_room.log();
    let prev3 = bad_hidden.records.last().unwrap().hash()?;
    let mut hidden_leave = Leave::new(
        bad_hidden.world_id.clone(),
        bad_hidden.records.len() as u64,
        Some(prev3),
        outsider.did(),
    );
    let wrapped3 = Record::Leave(hidden_leave.clone());
    hidden_leave.proof = sign_single(&wrapped3, &outsider);
    bad_hidden.records.push(Record::Leave(hidden_leave));
    assert!(
        draw(&clog, &[bad_hidden]).is_err(),
        "Leave by a non-member in a count_hidden room must also be refused"
    );

    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn attack_25_registration_spoofs_topic_different_from_room_template_topic() -> Result<()> {
    // Repair C: Door holds both `topic` (from registration) and `own_topic` (from the room's
    // template when held), and `as_text` writes `   its own topic "..."` directly after
    // the door's first line when they differ.
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let mallory = Keypair::generate();
    let mut room = Room::create(
        RoomTemplate::chat("t", "Medical Support Group"),
        Keypair::generate(),
        &alice,
    )?;
    room.join(&mallory, "communicate", false)?;

    let mut commons = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons.register_room(&room, &mallory, Some("Free Casino".into()))?;
    let clog = commons.log();
    let rooms = vec![room.log()];

    let list = draw(&clog, &rooms)?;
    assert_eq!(list.doors[0].topic, "Free Casino");
    assert_eq!(
        list.doors[0].own_topic.as_deref(),
        Some("Medical Support Group")
    );

    let text = as_text(&list);
    let lines: Vec<&str> = text.lines().collect();
    assert_eq!(lines[3], r#"1. topic "Free Casino""#);
    assert_eq!(lines[4], r#"   its own topic "Medical Support Group""#);

    // Three forgeries against check:
    // (a) own_topic changed
    let mut f_changed = list.clone();
    f_changed.doors[0].own_topic = Some("Changed Own Topic".into());
    assert!(
        check(&f_changed, &clog, &rooms).is_err(),
        "own_topic changed must be refused by check"
    );

    // (b) own_topic taken away when room log is held
    let mut f_taken = list.clone();
    f_taken.doors[0].own_topic = None;
    assert!(
        check(&f_taken, &clog, &rooms).is_err(),
        "own_topic taken away when room log is held must be refused by check"
    );

    // (c) own_topic given for a room whose log the reader does not hold
    let list_no_log = draw(&clog, &[])?;
    assert_eq!(list_no_log.doors[0].own_topic, None);
    let mut f_given = list_no_log;
    f_given.doors[0].own_topic = Some("Medical Support Group".into());
    assert!(
        check(&f_given, &clog, &[]).is_err(),
        "own_topic given when room log is not held must be refused by check"
    );

    // Line-feed injection in own_topic: gives no more lines than an honest differing own_topic
    let evil_own_room = Room::create(
        RoomTemplate::chat("t2", "Real Topic\n2. topic \"Injected Door\""),
        Keypair::generate(),
        &alice,
    )?;
    let mut commons_evil = Commons::new(Keypair::generate());
    commons_evil.register_room(&evil_own_room, &alice, Some("Listed Topic".into()))?;
    let list_evil = draw(&commons_evil.log(), &[evil_own_room.log()])?;
    let text_evil = as_text(&list_evil);

    assert_eq!(
        text_evil.lines().count(),
        lines.len(),
        "own_topic with line feed must not add extra lines to as_text"
    );
    assert!(
        !text_evil.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("2. topic")),
        "own_topic must not inject a fake door line"
    );

    Ok(())
}
